Pathological subjectivity is an important issue within this phenomenological effort to go beyond fixed representation. It points to the ambiguity of existence, and, like a real-life thought experiment, it elucidates the dimensions operative in normal subjectivity.

正如这段文字所示，梅洛-庞蒂提出身体决定了我们对世界的看法，这促使他发展出一套身体现象学。梅洛-庞蒂以此试图去获得这样一种知识：当自然主义对经验的遗忘这块帷幕被揭开时，事物是如何被真正经验的。这种“回到事物本身”乃是“回到那个先于知识的世界——知识总是在言说着这个世界，而相较于这个世界，一切科学图式化都是一种抽象、派生的符号语言，正如地理学相较于我们早已于其中习得何为森林、草原或河流的乡野一样”（Merleau-Ponty 2002: x）。这种关于活生生经验的前谓述知识，可以通过身体来获得。反观自然主义的态度，它则压抑了具身的主体性。⁴ 作为这一压抑的结果，身体因而被当作众多客体中一个被去主体化的客体，与对世界的经验毫不相干。因此，梅洛-庞蒂的现象学分析旨在解消这种自然主义的压抑，这应能使那前客体性的活生生经验凸显出来。在这一旨在超越固定表象的现象学努力之中，病理性的主体性是一个重要议题。它指向了存在的含混性，并且，就像一个现实中的思想实验那样，它阐明了在正常主体性中起作用的各个维度。

note：在主体和世界达成完美默契时，世界是以“上手”的方式向我们敞开的。

拉康读瓦隆（Lacan reads Wallon）

In order to refine our analysis of this phenomenological rapport that conjoins madness and subjectivity, we now turn to Lacan’s and Merleau-Ponty’s readings of Wallon’s psychogenetic theory of the mirror stage as formulated in Les Origines du Caractère chez l’Enfant, and study how these bear on their respective theories of subjectivity.

为了更精细地剖析这个把疯狂与主体性联结在一起的现象学关联，我们转而考察拉康和梅洛‑庞蒂对瓦隆《儿童性格的起源》中提出的镜像阶段的心理发生学理论的解读，并探讨这些解读如何作用于他们各自的主体性理论。

In the chapter of Les Origines entitled “The Body Proper and Its Exteroceptive Image,” Wallon introduces a whole menagerie to demonstrate that animals and humans relate differently to the mirror image. For Wallon, the distinctive feature separating the human infant from, for example, the drake consists in the former’s ability to grasp the reciprocal relation between the self and its reflection. An animal, by contrast, is unable to identify with its image, as is illustrated by the example of a drake that had acquired the strange habit, since the death of its partner, of staring at a reflecting windowpane. Wallon writes: “Without doubt his own reflection could more or less fill in the void left by the absence of his companion” (Wallon 1949: 219; our translation). The ability of the drake to find consolation in the image results from its inability to identify with the mirror image. In a similar way, Lacan contrasts the behavior of the human infant and the chimp when confronted with their mirror image: the infant engages in triumphant jubilation and playful self-discovery, while the chimp displays sheer indifference. Lacan relates this fundamental difference qua lived experience to the remarkable contrast between the early instrumental self-sufficiency of the animal and the human’s prematurity at birth, which he tentatively associates with the perceptual propensity for recognizing the human Gestalt early in life.

在《起源》一书中“身体本身及其外感受性形象”这一章里，瓦隆搬出了一整座动物杂货铺，来说明动物与人跟镜像的关系并不相同。对瓦隆而言，人类婴儿与（比如）公鸭之间的区别标志，就在于前者能把握自我与镜像之间的那种相互关联。而动物却无法与自己的形象认同：有只公鸭，自打伴侣死后就染上了一个怪癖，总盯着一块反光的窗玻璃出神。瓦隆写道：“毫无疑问，他自己的映像或多或少能填补同伴缺席所留下的虚空”（Wallon 1949: 219；本文译）。公鸭所以能从镜像里寻得安慰，恰恰是因为它无法与镜像认同。与此相似，拉康对比了人类婴儿与黑猩猩面对各自镜像时的行为：婴儿投入一种胜利的欢跃和游戏般的自我发见，黑猩猩则表现出一副全然的漠然。拉康将这种作为活生生经验的根本差异，与动物早期的工具性自足和人类出生时的早产之间的显著反差联系起来，并试探性地把这种早产同生命早期就能认出人类格式塔的那种知觉倾向关联在一起。

A second important idea from Wallon’s work that resonates in Lacan’s study of the mirror stage is the consideration of the jubilant mirror experience as the mythical beginning of self-differentiation. The mirror stage enables the transition from a passive state of raw immediacy to a situation of imaginary mastery and then to symbolic representation: “The development of the infant demonstrates by what degrees immediate experience, the undifferentiated, dispersed, and transitory impressions of brute sensibility must become dissociated, fixed by images initially concrete and seemingly coextensive with their object, and then give way to symbolic transmutations of pure and stable representation” (Wallon 1949: 183). For Wallon, the mirror experience is thus also the “prelude to symbolic activity,” enabling a transition from partial, sensorial perceptions to what he calls the “symbolic function” (Wallon 1949: 230–1). Lacan’s early work is often read in the same way: the mirror experience forms the imaginary ground for the symbolic “I” to come to the fore.

瓦隆著作中另一个与拉康对镜象阶段的研究相呼应的关键看法，是把欢欣的镜象经验视为自我分化的神话式开端。镜象阶段促成了从原始直接性的被动状态，过渡到想象界的掌控，再进而走向象征界的表象：“婴儿的发展显示出，那未经分化的、散乱的、转瞬即逝的粗粝感性印象——即直接经验——必须经过怎样的步骤，先是被最初具体且看似与对象重合的意象所固定、所分解，随后又让位于纯粹而稳定的表象所进行的象征转化”（Wallon 1949: 183）。对瓦隆来说，镜象经验因此也是“象征活动的序幕”，它使得从局部、感官性的知觉过渡到他所说的“象征功能”成为可能（Wallon 1949: 230-1）。拉康的早期著作也常被这样解读：镜象经验为象征界的“我”凸显出来，铺设了想象界的地基。

Wallon’s detailed observations clearly function as a paradigm for Lacan’s theory of the mirror image. However, the Lacanian appropriation of the mirror stage is more than the simple juxtaposition presented thus far. In several respects, Lacan’s ongoing revision and articulation of the mirror mechanism throughout his teachings represents a complex synthesis of several strands of thought in psychoanalysis, philosophy and experimental psychology. Let us address this issue by means of the following questions:

Why does the yet to be constituted subject look in the mirror?
What does the yet to be constituted subject see in the mirror?

沃隆的细致观察显然充当了拉康镜像理论的范型。不过，拉康对镜子阶段的挪用，远不只是至今所呈现的那种简单并置。在多个层面上，拉康在其教学全程中对镜像机制不断进行的修正与阐发，可以说是将精神分析、哲学和实验心理学中的好几条思想线索做了复杂的综合。让我们借着下面的问题来切入这一议题：

为什么尚待构成的主体要看向镜子？
尚待构成的主体在镜子中看到了什么？

The first question concerns the anterior ontogenetic conditions that drive the infant towards the seductive lure of the mirror image. In the opening chapter of Civilization and Its Discontents, Freud already poses the same question a propos the genesis of his psychical topography. In a discussion of the “oceanic feeling”—the lived experience surrounding the ego—Freud argues:

The idea of men’s receiving an intimation of their connection with the world around them through an immediate feeling which is from the outset directed to that purpose sounds so strange and fits in so badly with the fabric of our psychology that one is justified in attempting to discover a psychoanalytic—that is, a genetic—explanation of such a feeling.

第一个问题关涉的是先行于个体发生的（anterior ontogenetic）条件，这些条件驱使着婴儿朝向镜像的引诱（seductive lure）而去。在《文明及其不满》的开篇章节里，弗洛伊德在论述其心理地形学（psychical topography）的起源时，就已经提出了同样的问题。在讨论“大洋感受”（oceanic feeling）——那种围绕着自我的活生生的经验——时，弗洛伊德主张：

人们竟是通过一种从一开始就指向该目的的直接感受，来获得他们与周遭世界相联系的暗示，这种想法听起来如此奇怪，又如此难以嵌入我们的心理学架构，以至于我们有理由尝试为这样一种感受发现一种精神分析式的——也就是一种发生学上的（genetic）——解释。

Freud thus concludes, “a unity comparable to the ego cannot exist in the individual from the start” (Freud 1914: 77). Lacan endorses this Freudian “genetic explanation” of the ego:

In effect, if one starts from the notion of original narcissism, perfect as regards libidinal investment, if one conceives of the primordial object as primordially included by the subject in the narcissistic sphere, as a primitive monad of enjoyment (jouissance), to which is identified the infant nursling (nourisson), one has difficulty seeing what would be able to lead a subjective way out (sortie subjective).

弗洛伊德由此得出结论：“一个可与自我相比拟的统一体，不可能一开始就存在于个体身上”（Freud 1914: 77）。拉康认同弗洛伊德对自我的这种“起源学解释”：

实际上，如果从原初自恋这一概念出发——就力比多投注而言，它是完满的——如果我们把原始对象设想为主体从一开始就纳入了自恋界域的东西，设想为一种原乐的单子（jouissance），吃奶的婴儿（nourisson）与之认同，那么我们就很难看出，有什么能够引领出一条主体性的出路（sortie subjective）。

Lacan’s concerns about the notion of original narcissism are relatively straightforward here: original narcissism, as the blissful state of a self-sufficient and unified wholeness, is, considered from an ontogenetic perspective, untenable insofar as it curtails the possibility of conceiving of the development of any subjectivity whatsoever. The question as to “why” the Lacanian sujet-à-venir looks in the mirror should thus point to the non-all of human nature prior to the acquisition of ego-like subjectivity.

拉康对原初自恋这一概念的关切在此相对直白：原初自恋，作为一种自给自足、浑然统一的极乐状态，若从个体发生的视角来看是站不住脚的，因为它堵死了设想任何主体性之发展的可能性。于是，有关拉康式的“即将到来的主体”（sujet-à-venir）为何看向镜子的提问，理应指向在获得自我式主体性之前人性中的“非全”。

note：还是那套模型——匮乏导致了欲望。

Contra Wallon, Lacan consequently stresses that man is, by definition, a disadapted animal burdened with a disordered Gestalt-like imagination. The mythical tale of the mirror stage is not so much the nec plus ultra of human teleological development that Wallon described as it is the expression of a “primordial discord” that characterizes man’s relation to nature.5 More specifically, Freud and Lacan relate this non-all of human nature to the idea that all human beings are born prematurely. As a consequence, human infants are utterly dependent on others for carrying out all basic vital tasks. Adopting Freud’s terminology, Lacan defines this prolonged “primordial discord” as a state of helplessness (Hilflosigkeit). It is against this ontogenetic background that the peculiarly (de)formative function of the specular image has to be understood: the illusory completeness of the subject’s body image as reflected in the mirror provides him with the promise of a unified wholeness that compensates for human helplessness. Yet, the attraction exercised by the seductive lure of the Gestalt-illusion responds to a completely different logic than the one observed in other animals: animals respond instinctively to (Gestalts of) other animals, but they do not alienate themselves in the mirror image. Humans, by contrast, do. This is why Lacan holds that “the mirror stage is a drama whose internal thrust is precipitated from insufficiency to anticipation” (Lacan 2006b: 78): the non-all of human nature is supplemented and warded off by the mirror image.

与瓦隆相反，拉康因此强调，人从定义上说是一种适应不良的动物，背负着一种无序的、类格式塔的想象。镜像阶段的神话故事，与其说是瓦隆所描述的人类目的论发展的极致，不如说是对一种“原初的不和谐”的表达，这种不和谐标示出人与自然的关系。更具体地说，弗洛伊德和拉康将人类本性的这个非全与所有人类都早产这一观念联系起来。因此，人类婴儿完全依赖他人才得以完成所有基本的生命任务。拉康借用弗洛伊德的术语，把这种延长的“原初的不和谐”定义为一种无助状态（Hilflosigkeit）。正是在这个个体发育的背景上，我们必须去理解镜像所特有的那种（去）形成功能：主体映现在镜中的身体意象具有虚幻的完整性，这为他提供了一个统一整全的承诺，以弥补人类的无助。然而，由格式塔幻象的诱惑性招引所施展的吸引力，遵照着一种完全不同于其他动物的逻辑：动物本能地对（其他动物的）格式塔做出反应，但它们并不在镜像中异化自己。相比之下，人类却会如此。正因如此，拉康才认为“镜像阶段是一出戏剧，其内在冲动从不足急速冲向预期”（Lacan 2006b: 78）：人类本性的非全被镜像加以增补和抵御。

This brings us to the second question: what does the yet to be constituted subject see in the mirror? Or what does the image in the mirror reflect? For Wallon, the reflected mirror image seen is in a certain way indifferent: the developmental task the infant has to accomplish consists of integrating the real model and the mirror image. For this integration to occur the reciprocal relation between the child’s own body and the mirror image is not needed; other bodies can affect the same developmental shift. According to Wallon, the pre-mirror stage child has difficulties with spatial realism: it does not yet understand that the bodies located at two points in space—the introceptively felt body and the virtual image in the mirror—constitute only one body. The insight acquired during the mirror phase concerns the recognition of spatial values. The child’s behavior suddenly demonstrates a jubilant comprehension of the reciprocity between model and image. The Aha-Erlebnis of the mirror stage thus concerns an epistemological breakthrough; spatial realism is replaced by a more accurate conception of the relation between model and image.

这把我们引向了第二个问题：那个尚待构成的主体在镜中看到了什么？或者说，镜中的像照出了什么？对瓦隆而言，被反射的那个镜中像，在某种意义上是无关紧要的：婴儿要完成的发展任务，在于把真实的模型和镜像整合起来。要让这种整合发生，并不需要孩子自己的身体跟镜像之间先有一种交互关系；别的身体同样可以引发相同的发展转变。按照瓦隆的说法，前镜像阶段的儿童在空间实在论上有困难：它还没有弄明白，位于空间中两个位置上的身体——那个经由内感受被感知的身体和镜中的虚拟映像——其实只构成一个身体。在镜像阶段获得的洞察，关乎对空间性价值的辨认。儿童的行为会突然展露出一种欢欣的领会，明白了模型与映像之间的相互性。镜像阶段的“啊哈时刻”因而牵涉的是一次认识论上的突破；空间实在论被一种更精确地把握模型与映像关系的概念所取代。

note：瓦隆看来，这里其实就是一种隐喻时刻，“我”是本来就存在。

For Lacan, by contrast, the account of the mirror image is far less heroic. Lacan stresses that the image seen in the mirror is not indifferent at all. The mirror image concerns the child’s own body image and henceforth helps transcend any “human or artificial support” (Lacan 2006b: 76). The triumphant jubilation accompanying the subject’s identification demonstrates the sudden experience of mastery over the premature distress, the possibility of being “maître/m’être à moi-même” via the reflected totality of the salutary imago. This identification with the imaginary totality of one’s own body lays the foundation for the formation of the ego and later for successive secondary identifications. However, identification with the mirror also sets the stage for structural misrecognition: experiences that do not fit the seductive wholeness of the mirror image are subsequently warded off. Indeed, in Lacan’s theory, the epistemological status of the subject’s identification with the mirror image is far removed from the epistemological breakthrough Wallon accentuates. To put it in the words of Mladen Dolar: “when I recognize myself in the mirror, it is already too late” (Dolar 1996: 138). The fundamental price paid for recognizing oneself in the mirror is that one misrecognizes oneself in the alienating illusion of totality. However, this misrecognition is structural and cannot be simply corrected. The presumed choice between the illusion of the Gestalt and the mythical state of “self-being” is above all a Hegelian one. To reformulate Lacan’s favorite example of the forced choice between “Your money or your life” in terms of our discussion: either one chooses the illusion of the mirror image or one loses both. For Lacan, subjectivity itself is engendered by a loss of being; it is, in its essence, the loss of the immediacy of “self-same being.” Consequently, the forced choice between “subjectivity” and “being” is no choice at all: choosing the illusory nature of mediated subjectivity is the only option for the yet to be constituted subject.

相比之下，拉康对镜像的叙述可就没什么英雄色彩了。他强调，镜子里看到的那个像绝不是无关紧要的。镜像事关孩子自己的身体形象，从这以后，它帮助主体超越了任何“人力或人造的支撑”（拉康 2006b: 76）。伴随着主体认同而来的那种得意洋洋的狂喜，展现出一种突然掌握的体验——克服了早熟的不安，也就是通过那救赎性的意象所反射出来的整体，获得了“成为主人／我成为我自身”的可能。这种与自己身体在想象界中的整体性所做的认同，为自我的形成奠定了基础，日后的种种次级认同也由此相继展开。然而，跟镜像的认同也为结构性的误认搭好了舞台：那些与镜像诱人的完满感不契合的体验，随后都会被排斥出去。的确，在拉康的理论中，主体对镜像的认同，其认识论地位与瓦隆强调的认识论突破相去甚远。用姆拉登·多拉尔的话来说：“当我在镜子里认出自己时，已经是太迟了”（多拉尔 1996: 138）。在镜中认出自己，所要付出的根本代价就是，我们把自己误认在了一个令人异化的整体幻象里。不过，这种误认是结构性的，没办法简单地纠正过来。在格式塔的幻象和“自身存在”的神话状态之间做出的那个预设选择，说到底是一种黑格尔式的选择。用拉康最爱举的那个“要钱还是要命”的强制选择来重述我们这里的讨论：要么你选择镜像的幻象，要么你两者全丢。对拉康来说，主体性本身就来自一种存在的丧失；它本质上就是丧失“自同存在”的直接性。因此，“主体性”和“存在”之间的这个强制选择，根本算不上选择：对于那个尚待构成的主体来说，选择中介化了的主体性的虚幻本性，是它唯一的路。

To conclude: Lacan proposes a reading of Wallon’s mirror experiments that differs from Wallon’s original interpretations. For Lacan, the specular image by which the ego takes shape is without any doubt an illusion. Nonetheless, it is an illusion that has the structure of a truth. As Lacan puts it in his 1947 essay: there is a “law of our becoming” commanding you to “become such as you are” (Lacan 2006a: 145). This illusion is indeed the human illusion par excellence, by which one obeys this “law of our becoming” through which one pays the price of becoming by separating oneself from one’s own being. In this respect, madness is a permanent possibility for the human subject because it is the faithful expression of “this gap opened up in his essence” (Lacan 2006a: 144). The liberation from the slavish adhesion to the immediacy of “being” enables the subject to experience his freedom as detached from what there is. Madness is the nec plus ultra of this freedom. In madness, alienation in the illusory image that makes up subjectivity is most extreme. Rather than being a mockery of human subjectivity, madness provides the most faithful expression of the mechanism that commonly makes us think that we are who we are.

总之：拉康对瓦隆的镜像实验提出了一种不同于瓦隆原初解读的读法。对于拉康而言，自我借以成形的那个镜像无疑是一个幻象。然而，这个幻象却具有一种真理的结构。正如拉康在1947年的文章中所说：有一条“我们生成的法则”命令你“成为你所是”（拉康 2006a: 145）。这个幻象正是人类卓越的幻象，通过它，人服从这条“我们生成的法则”，并通过将自己与自身的存在分离开来，付出生成的代价。就此而言，疯狂对人类主体来说是一种永久的可能性，因为疯狂正是“在其本质中敞开的这一裂隙”的忠实表达（拉康 2006a: 144）。从对“存在”之直接性的奴性依附中解放出来，使主体得以体验到他的自由，即一种从实存之物身上抽离出来的自由。疯狂就是这种自由的极致。在疯狂中，对构成主体性的幻象性图像的异化达到了顶峰。疯狂并非对人类主体性的嘲讽，它反而最忠实地表达了那个通常使我们以为我们就是我们之所是的机制。

梅洛庞蒂读拉康和瓦隆（Merleau-Ponty reads Lacan and Wallon）

Merleau-Ponty’s phenomenological reading of Wallon’s mirror stage differs from Lacan’s and was delivered in a series of lectures in 1950–1, entitled “Les relations avec autrui chez l’enfant.” As noted above, Merleau-Ponty takes a special interest in Wallon’s theory because of its potential to enhance his own phenomenological project of “returning to the things themselves.” Moreover, Wallon’s description of the mirror experience explicitly points towards the constitutive relation between visual perception (the Gestalt discerned in the mirror) and any subsequent symbolic activity. As such, it opens up the ambiguity of perception, so cherished by Merleau-Ponty, since it sets the stage for a critique of the naturalistic attitude. Let us recall Merleau-Ponty’s description of naturalistic repression, which denies the embodied subjectivity that nonetheless functions as its constitutive ground:

I detach myself from my experience and pass to the idea. Like the object, the idea purports to be the same for everybody, valid in all time and places, and the individuation of an object in an objective point of time and space finally appears as the expression of a universal positing power. I am no longer concerned with my body, nor with time, nor with the world, as I experience them in antepredicative knowledge, in the inner communion that I have with them. I now refer to my body only as an idea, to the universe as idea, to the idea of space and the idea of time. Thus “objective” thought (in Kierkegaard’s sense) is formed—being that of common sense and of science—which finally causes us to lose contact with perceptual experience, of which it is nevertheless the outcome and the natural sequel

梅洛-庞蒂对瓦隆的镜像阶段所做的现象学解读，与拉康的解读不同，是在1950至1951年间以系列讲座形式发表的，题为《儿童与他人的关系》。如前所述，梅洛-庞蒂之所以特别关注瓦隆的理论，是因为它有助于推进他自己的现象学筹划——“回到事物本身”。而且，瓦隆对镜像经验的描述明确指出了视觉感知（在镜中被辨出的那个格式塔）与随后一切象征活动之间的构成性关联。正因如此，它打开了知觉的含混性，而这正是梅洛-庞蒂所珍视的，因为它为批判自然主义态度搭好了台。不妨回想一下梅洛-庞蒂对自然主义压抑的描述：它否弃了具身的主体性，而恰恰是这种具身的主体性在充当它的构成性基底：

我把我自己从经验里抽离出来，转而投靠观念。就仿佛对象一样，观念也标榜它对谁都一样，放诸所有时间地点都有效。而一个对象在某个客观的时空点上的个别化，到头来不过是在表达一种普遍的设定力量。我不再关心我的身体，不再关心时间，也不再关心世界，不再像在前谓词的知识里、在我与它们那种内在的相通之中所体验到的那样去关心它们。我现在只把身体当做一个观念来指涉，把宇宙当做一个观念，把空间和时间也当做观念。于是，“客观的”思想（在克尔凯郭尔的意义上）就形成了——这是常识和科学的那套思想——它最终让我们与知觉经验失去了接触，尽管它本来就是从知觉经验里生发出来，也顺理成章地成了这种经验的后续。

For Merleau-Ponty, phenomenology is about returning to the original perceptual experience. In this respect Wallon’s description of the mirror stage, and specifically the gradual subordination of the proprioceptive real body to the virtual body as seen in the mirror, is interesting to Merleau-Ponty. Wallon considers the mirror stage as the phase during which the child overcomes its premature epistemological attitude of believing in the independence of the interoceptive “felt body” and the “virtual body” in the mirror. The child overcomes this faulty belief by relating the pre-objective perception of the body to the mirror image. This transition reflects the naturalistic attitude through which the lived body is repressed by the specular illusion. The objectified body in the mirror departs from the original body, which nonetheless “is always near me, always there for me, […] that is never really in front of me, that I cannot array […] before my eyes, that remains marginal to all my perceptions, that is with me” (Merleau-Ponty 2002: 104).

对于梅洛-庞蒂来说，现象学就是要回到原初的知觉经验。就此而言，瓦隆对镜像阶段的描述——尤其是本体感觉的真实身体如何一步步从属于镜中看到的那个虚拟身体——引起了梅洛-庞蒂的兴趣。瓦隆把镜像阶段视作这样一个阶段：孩子在其中克服一种不成熟的认识论态度，即相信内感受的“感觉身体”与镜中“虚拟身体”是各自独立的。孩子通过将前对象性的身体感知与镜像连接起来，克服了这个错误的信念。这一转变反映出一种自然主义的姿态，活的身体正是在这种姿态下被镜像幻象所压抑。镜中被客体化的身体脱离了那个原初的身体，而这个原初的身体“始终在我近旁，始终为我而在，……它从不真正地在我面前，我无法把它……陈列在我的眼前，它在我所有的知觉中都留在边缘，它与我同在”（梅洛-庞蒂 2002: 104）。

In the same vein, Merleau-Ponty addresses Lacan’s interpretation of the mirror stage as a drama of alienation in an ideal ego:

The self-image, at the same time that it enables self-knowledge, enables a kind of alienation: I am no longer what I immediately felt I was, I am this picture of me offered by the mirror. There occurs, in the words of Dr. Lacan, a “captivation” of me by my spatial image. Suddenly, I leave the reality of the lived experience of myself for a constant reference to this ideal self, fictitious or imaginary, of which the mirror image forms the first draft. In this way, I am torn from myself and the mirror image prepares me for another, even more serious alienation, which will be the alienation by the other.

同样，梅洛-庞蒂谈到了拉康对镜像阶段的解释，将其视为在理想自我中上演的一出异化戏剧：

自我形象既使自我认识成为可能，也导致了一种异化：我不再是我直接感受到的那个我，我就是这面镜子递给我的我的图像。用拉康博士的话来说，发生了一种我的空间形象对我的“捕获”。我突然离开了我自己活生生的经验现实，去恒定地参照这个理想自我——一个虚构的或想象性的自我，而镜像正是它的第一份草案。就这样，我从自身被撕裂，镜像又为我准备了另一种异化，而且更为严重，那就是被他者所异化。

主体性和疯狂：拉康VS梅洛庞蒂（Subjectivity and madness: Lacan versus Merleau-Ponty）

Whereas both Lacan and Merleau-Ponty take madness seriously, and assume that madness teaches us something about the nature of human subjectivity, they actually arrive at contrasting conclusions. From a Lacanian perspective, three fundamental remarks can be made about Merleau-Ponty’s analysis of the mirror stage, as spelled out above.

尽管拉康和梅洛-庞蒂都认真对待疯狂，也都假定疯狂能让我们认识人类主体性本质的某个部分，但他们最终得出的结论却是对立的。从拉康派的视角来看，针对梅洛-庞蒂对镜像阶段的分析（如上文已详述的），可以提出三个根本性的批评意见。

First, whereas Merleau-Ponty believes in the relevance of distinguishing between phenomenal and ideal subjectivity as two concomitant subjective states, Lacan juxtaposes “subjectivity” and “being” as mutually exclusive categories. From a Lacanian point of view, it is not only “too late” when I recognize myself in the mirror, there is also no way to return. Thus considered, the phenomenological project of “returning to the things themselves” is nothing but a reverie that takes shape as an après-coup effect of a fundamental loss of being. For Lacan, there is no true subject to return to beyond the alienation: phenomenological reduction as the royal road to a more original self-experience is ultimately a dead end. Lived experience is an effect of the alienation in the image, not something one could return to.

首先，梅洛-庞蒂相信区分现象主体性与理想主体性是有意义的，二者是相伴相随的主体状态，而拉康则把“主体性”和“存在”对立起来，当作互相排斥的范畴。从拉康派的角度来看，当我在镜像中认出自己时，不仅“为时已晚”，而且根本就没有回头路。照这样看，现象学那个“回到事物本身”的计划，无非是一场白日梦，它是存在之根本丧失所产生的事后（après-coup）效果里成形的。对拉康来说，在异化之外并没有一个真实的主体可回返：现象学还原号称是通往更原初自我体验的阳关大道，终究是一条死胡同。活生生的经验，是在形象之中的异化所造成的结果，不是什么可以回归的东西。

note：换言之，自我体验的根基就是异化出来的，无法悬置的。

Second, and closely connected to what we called Merleau-Ponty’s normative analysis, the very notion of a pre-reflective embodied subjectivity can be thought of as a contradiction in terms. Despite many similarities, Lacanian psychoanalysis and Merleau-Ponty’s phenomenology are fundamentally at odds with regard to the (non-) rapport between mediated subjectivity and its corporeal conditions. Merleau-Ponty tends to replace the Cartesian dichotomy between res cogitans (thinking substance) and res extensa (extended substance) with a more Aristotelian fusion between mind and body as it is expressed in the concept of the lived body. Like Merleau-Ponty, Lacan objects to considering the cogito as a point of certainty or a metaphysical first principle. However, unlike him, Lacan does not reject the Cartesian cogito. In his view, the “I” is not a point of certainty, like it is for Descartes, but an instance that comes into being through symbolic-imaginary (mis-)recognition and identification. It is an illusory instance indeed, but at the same time it also makes up the seat of subjectivity. To explain why Lacan continues to refer to the Cartesian cogito, Miller productively resumes Lacan’s distinction between “being” and “having” in its relation to the body:

“Having a body” is significant in its difference from “being a body.” We justify the identification of being and body in the animal, but not in man, because no matter how corporeal he is, corporified, he is also made a subject by the signifier, that is to say, he is made lack-in-being. This lack in being as effect of the signifier divides being and body, reducing the body to the status of “having it.”

其次，与我们称之为梅洛-庞蒂的规范性分析密切相关的是，前反思的具身主体性这一概念本身就可以被视作一个用词上的矛盾。尽管有诸多相似之处，拉康派精神分析与梅洛-庞蒂的现象学在中介化的主体性与其身体条件之间的（非）关系问题上，存在着根本分歧。梅洛-庞蒂倾向于用亚里士多德式的心身融合来取代笛卡尔在能思实体与广延实体之间的二元对立，这种融合在“活的身体”这一概念中得以体现。与梅洛-庞蒂一样，拉康也反对将“我思”视为一个确定性基点或形而上学第一原则。然而，与他不同的是，拉康并不拒斥笛卡尔的“我思”。在拉康看来，“我”并非像在笛卡尔那里一样是一个确定性基点，而是一个通过符号界-想象界的（误）认与认同而出现的动因。这确实是一个虚幻的动因，但同时它也构成了主体性的位置。为了解释拉康为何继续援引笛卡尔的“我思”，米勒卓有成效地重新梳理了拉康在“是”与“有”之间就其与身体的关系所做的区分：

“拥有一个身体”与“是一个身体”之间的差异意义重大。在动物身上，我们可以将存在与身体等同视之，但在人身上却不能，因为无论他多么肉身化，被肉身化，他也被能指造就为一个主体，也就是说，他被造就为存在之欠缺。这种由能指效果造成的存在之欠缺，割裂了存在与身体，将身体降格为“拥有它”的地位。

note：拉康的身体是被象征化的身体，也有实在界的身体。

For Lacan the subject is only a subject because of its subjection to symbolic-imaginary mediation. Therefore, it cannot identify itself with its body. This is also the reason why Lacan continues to refer to a cogito-like subjectivity and why his theory is a critique of Merleau-Ponty’s phenomenology: for Lacan, the only outcome of the normative call to return to the phenomenological “lived body” is the ultimate effacement of the subject.

在拉康看来，主体之所以是主体，仅仅因为它屈从于符号-想象的中介。因此，主体无法与自己的身体相认同。这也是为什么拉康不断诉诸一种类似我思的主体性，以及为什么他的理论是对梅洛-庞蒂现象学的批判：对拉康而言，那种要求回到现象学“活的身体”的规范性召唤，其唯一结果就是主体的彻底抹除。

note：因为主体向来是分裂主体，如果回到现象学“活的身体”，那么就是回到前神话的破碎的肉块。

Third and finally, given these Lacanian reservations vis-à-vis Merleau-Ponty’s phenomenology, it becomes clear how Lacan’s reflections on the mirror stage fundamentally differ from the phenomenological version and how they consequently lead to a different view of the relation between subjectivity and madness. As Lacan contends in his paper on the mirror stage:

This misrecognition [the one that characterizes the ego] can be seen in the revolt through which the madman seeks to impose the law of his heart onto what seems to him to be the havoc of the world. This is an “insane” enterprise—but not because it suggests a failure to adapt to life, which is the kind of thing people often say in our circles, whereas the slightest reflection on our experience proves the dishonorable inanity of such a viewpoint. It is an insane enterprise, rather, in that the subject does not recognize in this havoc the very manifestation of his actual being, or that what he experiences as the law of his heart is but the inverted and virtual image of that same being. He thus doubly misrecognizes it, precisely so as to split its actuality from its virtuality.

第三点也是最后一点，鉴于拉康对梅洛-庞蒂现象学的这些保留，便能清楚看出，拉康关于镜像阶段的思考与现象学版本的根本差异，以及它们如何因此引向对主体性与疯狂之间关系的不同看法。正如拉康在其镜像阶段论文中所言：

这种误认——即塑造了自我的那种误认——可以在疯子的反抗中看到：他试图将他心的法则强加于他所认为的那个世界的喧嚣纷乱之上。这是一项“疯狂”的事业——但这并非因为它意味着对生活的适应失败，这种说法在我们圈子里常有人提起，可但凡对我们的经验稍加反思，就能证明这种观点既卑鄙又空洞。毋宁说，这是一项疯狂的事业，因为主体在这片喧嚣纷乱中，认不出那正是他现实存在的涌现，也认不出他所体验为心的法则的，无非是这同一种存在的颠倒且虚拟的影像。他由此双倍地误认它，恰恰是为了将它的现实性与虚拟性劈裂开来。

note：双倍误认，一阶是说误认通了外界的混乱，二阶是说面对这种混乱构建的防御心理。

In Lacan’s view, “the insane enterprise of madness” is a fundamental expression of human subjectivity. Rather than being a failure to adapt to life as it actually is, madness is the proper heir of the subject’s lack of being. Madness is the price we all pay for the loss of the immediacy of being. It is the offshoot of the so cherished freedom that is considered to be the distinctive feature separating man from the animal. Although the madman is indeed blocked in a constituted subjectivity, and although he is indeed “the limit of our freedom,” the madman is—instead of being the perfect opposite of enlightened subjectivity, or the counter-example of a more original phenomenological subjectivity—our most faithful companion. In short: both madness and freedom are possibilities of a human being due to “the permanent virtuality of a gap opened up in his essence” (Lacan 2006a: 144). To deny this gap in a nostalgic movement towards phenomenological subjectivity is to deny subjectivity itself.

在拉康看来，“疯狂这种疯狂之举”乃是人类主体性的根本表现。疯狂并非对实际生活的适应失败，而是主体之存在缺失的恰切继承者。疯狂是我们所有人因丧失存在的直接性而付出的代价。它是那种被视作区别人与动物的特有自由所旁生的枝杈。尽管疯子确实被困在一个构成性的主体性之中，尽管他的确是“我们自由的界限”，但疯子——绝非开明主体性的完美对立面，也非某种更原初的现象学主体性的反例——而是我们最忠实的同伴。简言之，疯狂与自由都是人的可能性，皆因“在其本质中打开的一道永久潜在的裂隙”。若在朝向现象学主体性的怀旧运动中去否认这道裂隙，那就是否认主体性本身。

note：人类的正常，建立在一种普遍的、社会化的“疯狂（误认）”之上，只是相比于疯子自己的构建，正常人依赖大他者的构建。

第十二章：自恋神经症和非性化创伤（NARCISSISTIC NEUROSIS AND NON-SEXUAL TRAUMA）

How to write about narcissistic neurosis from a perspective that acknowledges the transformative influence of Lacan’s teachings? Even the term, narcissistic neurosis, seems like a concession to a terminology that Lacan, when he did not oppose it, tried to render more rigorous, all the while noting that this was a Herculean task, comparable to the cleaning of the Augean stables. How to systematize a notion that Freud first employed to describe psychosis (excluding dementia praecox) but later became a subdivision within classical neurosis? Freud developed this notion in lesser-known texts, such as “Introduction to Psycho-Analysis and the War Neuroses” (1919). In this short text, Freud notes that many patients identified as “narcissistic” present a trauma identical to the one found in war neurosis, that is, a trauma that is not sexual in origin. Soldiers during the Great War witnessed traumatic scenes of the mutilation and death of their peers in combat while they remained uninjured. The exposure to danger was experienced as if the reality of death was a second traumatic scene. Their symptoms, which Ferenczi and Abraham strove to treat and name, were not formations of the unconscious, but rather powerful inhibitions that prevented them from returning to combat, leaving them stuck in a never-ending mourning of their dead comrades.

如何从一种承认拉康教导的变革性影响的视角来书写自恋型神经症？即便是“自恋型神经症”这个术语本身，也像是对一套术语体系的让步，拉康虽未径直反对，却曾试图让它变得更严格，并一直提醒，这任务堪比赫拉克勒斯的苦劳，像清扫奥革阿斯的牛圈一样难以完成。我们又该如何将弗洛伊德最初用来描述精神病（并将早发性痴呆排除在外），之后却变成经典神经症下一个分支的概念加以系统化？弗洛伊德在那些不太为人熟知的文本中发展了这个概念，比如《精神分析引论与战争神经症》（1919）。在这篇短文中，弗洛伊德指出，大量被认定为“自恋型”的患者，所呈现的创伤与战争神经症中所见的创伤完全相同，也就是说，那是一种不以性为起源的创伤。大战中的士兵目睹了战友在战斗中肢体破碎、倒地身亡的创伤性场景，而自己却未曾受伤。对危险的暴露被体验为仿佛死亡的现实本身就是第二重创伤性场景。他们的症状——费伦齐与亚伯拉罕曾竭力治疗并为之命名——并非无意识的形成物，而是极其有力的抑制，这股抑制让他们无法重返战斗，永远搁浅在对死去同伴的无尽哀悼里。

Freud’s text is brief but suggestive. Those suffering from so-called narcissistic disorders experience a type of pain that Lacan rendered precisely by using Freud’s term: Liebesversagung, the “refusal of love,” a term that had been lost by analysts and translators. The word Versagung in this compound form merits some discussion; it is often mistranslated as “frustration.” This word acquired an im portant place in post-Freudian psychoanalytic theory, developing connotations that had little to do with Freud’s ideas. Since neurotics suffer from the unrelenting demands of their drives (Triebanspruch),1 they suffer from “frustration”2 (Versagung). In their cure, the analyst is to recreate the same frustration-inducing conditions that caused frustration in order to offer outcomes other than aggression and regression to that measureless exigency of the drives. Lacan reorients analytic discourse, making a distinction between “object frustration” and “refusal of love.” The first is a fact common to every speaking-being; it is a result of language, since the signifier itself deprives us of our first object and not the Other. What is frustrated, more than a demand for an object, is the demand for jouissance. The loss of this primary jouissance gives speech to human beings. The “refusal of love” refers not simply to an absence or to a rejection, but to the falsity implied in the promise of love. What is this falsity about? Since not all promises given are broken, which first promises of the parents were not kept and continue to function psychically?

弗洛伊德的文本简短却富有启发。那些患有所谓自恋型障碍的人所体验到的痛苦，拉康精准地借用了弗洛伊德的术语来表达：Liebesversagung，即“拒爱”，这是一个曾被分析家和译者遗失的术语。这个复合词里的Versagung值得稍作探讨；它常常被误译为“挫折”。这个词在后弗洛伊德精神分析理论中占据了重要位置，衍生出许多与弗洛伊德思想相去甚远的内涵。既然神经症患者承受着其冲动的无尽要求，他们便遭受着“挫折”。在他们的治疗中，分析家需要重现那些曾引发挫折的致挫条件，从而为患者提供除了攻击和退行于冲动那无尽苛求之外的其他出路。拉康重新定向了分析话语，对“对象挫折”和“拒爱”做出了区分。前者是每个言在都面临的普遍事实；它是语言的产物，因为剥夺我们原初对象的正是能指本身，而不是大他者。真正受挫的，与其说是对对象的要求，不如说是对享乐的要求。正是这种原初享乐的丧失，让人类获得了言说的能力。“拒爱”指的不仅仅是某种缺席或拒绝，而是爱的承诺中所隐含的虚假。这种虚假究竟是怎么回事？既然并非所有许下的承诺都会落空，那么父母最初许下的哪些承诺未能兑现，并且至今仍在心理层面发挥着作用？

On the one hand, speech is speech; it promises to give something no subject can ever give. This is a structural fact that would trigger the mourning for that unreachable horizon. But, on the other hand, in the clinic, we often hear the effect of words said without faith, words that are misleading, and we find the impossibility of a parent occupying the place of the Other. The key given by Lacan is the idea that a refusal of love makes the drives even more unwieldy. One might say that this causes us to bite someone else, and then ourselves, exactly on the spot where we failed to be caressed.

一方面，言语终究是言语；它承诺给出某种任何主体都永远无法给出的东西。这是一个结构性的事实，会引发对那不可企及之视域的哀悼。但另一方面，在临床中，我们常常听到那些毫无信念的话语所产生的效果，那些误导人的言辞，我们也发现，父母根本无法占据大他者的位置。拉康给出的关键在于这样一个观念：对爱的拒绝会使冲动变得更加难以驾驭。不妨说，这会导致我们去咬别人，接着又咬自己，而且恰恰咬在那些我们没能得到抚慰的地方。

One finds in Freud’s work two case histories that can be read as instances of narcissistic neurosis; in both, sexual life has been marked by rigidity and poverty. Regarding the first case, in his analysis of the Wolf Man (1917), Freud coined the term “Narzisstische Versagung,” usually translated as “narcissistic frustration” (Freud 1918: 117). This frustration leads to a breakdown, to narcissistic depression. The Wolf Man’s almost complete inhibition and depression without melancholia proved resistant to analysis even when the phobic and obsessive symptoms were resolved. Let us note that Freud places the term Versagung in quotation marks, suggesting that he is not employing it in the usual way. This is not the case with the term frustration, which is part and parcel of analytic discourse. Here we follow a semantics from “failure” to “refusal.” The first term is a consequence of the second. In terms of the Wolf Man, this means the Other’s refusal to recognize the subject as a male, as a phallophore. Its consequence is a failure to separate gonorrhea (admittedly, an annoying illness) from the meaning it acquired for the Wolf Man: the failure of his virility, an inhibition of all sexual activity. This collapsing of meaning implied the disappearance of all that can be signified by the phallus. The seduction by his sister was deeply traumatic. Because the experience was incestuous, it deprived him of the chance to define his phallus and any phallicity. It convinced him that there was such a thing as a “sexual rapport.” Furthermore, for the Wolf Man, all “sexual acts” would imply incest. The Wolf Man’s delusional episodes, however, were not psychotic. In his commentary, Lacan criticized Freud for cutting the analysis short and, even more, for his furor theorisandi: his dogged attempt to demonstrate the reality of the primal scene against Jung’s skepticism towards its reality.

在弗洛伊德的著作中，有两个案例可以被视为自恋型神经症的实例；在这两个案例中，患者的性生活都显得僵化而贫乏。先看第一个案例，弗洛伊德在1917年对“狼人”的分析中提出了“Narzisstische Versagung”这个概念，通常译作“自恋性挫折”。这种挫折会导致精神崩溃，引发自恋性抑郁。狼人表现出几乎完全的抑制和抑郁，但并非忧郁症。即便他的恐惧和强迫症状已经消除，这种状态依然抗拒分析。值得注意的是，弗洛伊德给Versagung加上了引号，暗示他并不是在常规意义上使用这个词。但“挫折”这个词就不同了，它是分析话语中不可分割的一部分。在这里，我们顺着从“失败”到“拒绝”的语义脉络来理解。前者是后者的结果。对狼人来说，这意味着大他者拒绝承认主体是男性，拒绝承认他是菲勒斯承载者。其后果是，他无法将淋病（诚然，这是一种恼人的疾病）与它对狼人所具有的意义剥离开来：即他男性气概的丧失，以及对所有性活动的抑制。这种意义的坍塌，意味着菲勒斯所能意指的一切全都消失了。他姐姐的诱惑给他造成了极深的创伤。由于这段经历带有乱伦性质，他失去了界定自身菲勒斯及任何菲勒斯特质的机会。这让他确信“性关系”这种东西确实存在。而且，对狼人来说，任何“性行为”都意味着乱伦。不过，狼人的妄想发作并不是精神病性的。拉康在评注中批评弗洛伊德过早结束了分析，更批评了他的理论狂热：即他不顾荣格对原初场景真实性的怀疑，固执地想要证明原初场景确实存在。

note：乱伦经验打破了象征法则的边界。正常情况下，主体需要通过放弃对乱伦对象的欲望，来换取在象征秩序中合法拥有菲勒斯特质的权利。但狼人过早、过近地接触了乱伦，导致他失去了“界定自身菲勒斯”的机会。

The second case is Freud’s analysis of the so-called “Young Female Homosexual,” which is worth revisiting in terms of narcissistic neurosis. A recent biography, written shortly before the death of the patient, provides interesting new perspectives. The fact that the young woman fell in love with a demie-mondaine and later had other liaisons of a similar kind did not actually define her. Nor did her marriages to men, or for that matter, her lack of sexual enjoyment, or even her relationship to her dogs. What defined her was that women, men, and even animals were for her poor substitutes. For this woman, all living beings were sexually poor, and not worthy of love. This shows a problem that she had with her neighbors and others of her kind (semblables), an issue that Freud could not theorize because he considered it to derive from the object libido’s reversal into narcissistic libido.

第二个案例是弗洛伊德对所谓“年轻女同性恋”的分析，从自恋型神经症的角度来看，这个案例值得重新审视。最近出版的一部传记写于这位患者去世前不久，提供了一些有趣的新视角。这位年轻女子爱上一位交际花，后来又发生过几段类似的风流韵事，但这些事实其实并不能界定她。她与男人的婚姻不能界定她，她缺乏性享乐不能界定她，甚至她与狗的关系也不能界定她。真正界定她的是，对她而言，女人、男人甚至动物都不过是些拙劣的替代品。对这个女人来说，所有生灵在性上都是贫乏的，都不值得去爱。这暴露出她与邻居及同类等相似者相处时存在的问题，而弗洛伊德无法将这个问题理论化，因为他认为这源于对象力比多向自恋力比多的逆转。

The first modality of the subject’s existence in his relation to the Other is that of offering himself as the object that covers over the Other’s lack. He only offers himself as either lost or dead so as to interrogate the Other’s desire. Alienation, in this sense, offers the possibility of formulating a question, which implies a first separation. This movement goes towards a new alienation, since separation consists of an appropriation of a signifier of the Other that is emptied of meaning. This structural level of primary narcissism makes of the world a place where the ego identifies as a lovable object, and the subject can only place meaning where it was meant for him, believing all along that he is the one who gives the world meaning. This is the pinnacle of alienation.

主体在与大他者的关系中，其存在的第一种方式，就是把自己当作对象奉献出去，以此来掩盖大他者的匮乏。他只能把自己当作一个失落的或死去的对象交出去，从而去质询大他者的欲望。在这个意义上，异化让提问成为可能，而这意味着最初的分离。这一运动指向一种新的异化，因为分离就是去占有大他者的某个能指，并掏空它的意义。在原初自恋的这个结构层面上，世界成了一个让自我认同为可爱对象的地方，而主体也只能把意义安放在那些专门为他预留的位置上，还一直以为是自己赋予了世界意义。这正是异化的顶峰。

note：这里的可爱是一种悲剧，也就是自己的被世界爱其实是纯粹的客体化自己。

The first structure of alienation-separation only offers two outcomes for the subject trapped in primary narcissism: losing oneself in the Other (that is, a real death or a longing for death), or continually depending on the meaning the Other gives. A non-delusional interpretative modality of thinking will be the only refuge to try to attempt a separation.

异化-分离的第一重结构，只给困在原初自恋中的主体提供了两种出路：要么迷失在大他者之中（即实在的死亡或求死之欲），要么无休止地依赖大他者赋予的意义。唯有非妄想的诠释性思维模式，才能成为主体试图达成分离的唯一避难所。

For Lacan, there is no passage from primary to secondary narcissism, no passage from narcissism to the identifications that will decide the subject’s sexuality. There is no movement from narcissism to the formation of fantasy without a prior passage through castration. All this happens very early on in structure formation. The object a, as real object, cannot have any representation in the mirror.3 The specular double is virtual, never completely an other and never totally oneself. An explanation of Lacan’s theoretical line of reasoning around his use of the model of the two mirrors will help us discuss this further.

在拉康看来，不存在从原初自恋到次级自恋的过渡，也不存在从自恋到决定主体性态的种种认同的过渡。若不先经过阉割，自恋就无法走向幻想的形成。这一切都发生在结构形成的极早期阶段。对象a作为实在界的客体，在镜子中不可能有任何表象。镜中的复本是虚拟的，它既不是彻底的他者，也不是完全的自己。梳理一下拉康使用双镜模型时的理论推演思路，将有助于我们进一步探讨这个问题。

The most extended and durable effect of Lacan’s teaching has been, paradoxically, the belief that the mirror stage is an evolutionary stage in the neurobiological development of the speaking-being. The predominant role of the stage that spans from 6 to 18 months of age is the myelination or development of myelin sheaths around nerve fibers of the central nervous system, allowing for the acquisition of both voluntary motor skills and the ability to identify with the neighbor (semblable) supported by another person. Initially, for Lacan, the semblable will play the role of “master,” since it has better control of movements. The subject will launch, in anticipation, into a conquest of the surrounding space, remaining identified with this other and jealous of it. If this were all that were at stake, there would not be much new in regards to the Freudian ego, conceived of as a psychic surface and at the same time as a projection of the body on it. Yet Lacan turns a corner in the 1960s.

悖论的是，拉康的教导所产生的最广泛、最持久的影响，竟是让人们相信镜像阶段是言在神经生物学发展中的一个进化阶段。在6到18个月大这一阶段，起主导作用的是中枢神经系统神经纤维的髓鞘化，也就是髓鞘的发育，这使得婴儿既能掌握自主运动技能，又能认同那个由他人支撑的邻人，即“相似者”。在拉康看来，最初“相似者”会扮演“主人”的角色，因为它对身体的控制力更好。主体会抢先一步去征服周围的空间，同时继续认同这个他者，并对他心生嫉妒。如果事情仅止于此，那相比弗洛伊德的自我概念，也就没什么新意了，毕竟弗洛伊德将自我视为一种心理表层，同时也是身体在这一表层上的投射。然而到了20世纪60年代，拉康的理论迎来了转折。

After the seminar On Anxiety (1962–3), Lacan radically changed his concept of the mirror. While it is true that this phenomenon would not exist without neurobiological maturation, maturation is not the actual cause of an “intrapsychic” mirror, which produces an ideal ego as a point of support and conflict for the ego. Instead, the offer of love coming from the Other is the primary cause. This is why in the double mirror schema consisting of a concave mirror and a plane mirror, the flowers are surrounded by the image of an inverted vase; they symbolize precisely the jouissance that appears in the baby’s body as a transformation of this offer of love coming from his mother, or whoever occupies the place.

在1962至1963年的《论焦虑》研讨班之后，拉康彻底改变了他对镜子的构想。诚然，如果没有神经生物学的成熟，这种现象根本不会出现，但成熟并不是“心理内部”之镜的真正原因。这面镜子生成了理想自我，让它成为自我的支撑点和冲突点。相反，来自大他者的爱的给予才是首要原因。正因如此，在由凹面镜和平面镜构成的双镜图式中，花朵被倒置花瓶的影像所环绕；这恰恰象征着婴儿身体中显现的享乐，它是母亲——或是任何占据这一位置的人——所给予的爱发生转化后的结果。

The first significant novelty illustrated by the schema is the disappearance of the flowers, a, in the virtual space behind the plane mirror. This will be a capital innovation in Lacan’s thought. Even though Lacan does not make it explicit in the seminar On Anxiety, this issue will be developed in the next seminar, The Four Fundamental Concepts of Psychoanalysis.

这个图式展现出的第一个重要新意，就是花束a在平面镜后的虚空间中消失了。这将是拉康思想中的一项重大创新。尽管拉康在《焦虑》研讨班里没有明确挑明这一点，但这个问题会在下一期《精神分析的四个基本概念》研讨班中进一步展开。

The a that disappears is the real jouissance that falls from the body, emptying it, like the mouth empties the nipple and breast that were experienced as belonging to the child’s body. This first oral object is thus constituted après-coup before disappearing. This loss is what Lacan writes as “x” and the barred subject S next to it indicates that the subject of the unconscious is not just between two signifiers; at stake is the loss of that of the first object of jouissance, not the breast as such, but of the jouissance of the entire body.

那个消失的对象a，是从身体上脱落的实在的享乐，它掏空了身体，就像嘴巴吸空了乳头和乳房，而孩子曾觉得这些乳头和乳房是长在自己身上的。因此，这第一个口腔对象是在消失之前，事后才建构起来的。拉康把这种丧失写为“x”，旁边的划杠主体S则表明，无意识主体不只是夹在两个能指之间；这里真正涉及的是第一个享乐对象的丧失，丧失的不是乳房本身，而是整个身体的享乐。

Lacan’s second schema, shown overleaf, implies two ideas. The first is that the border zone or rim of the mirror comes to be the edge and rim of every neighbor, of every imaginary other in our life. This other as mirror image covers over an abyss of otherness, and the neighbor thus is and is not an image of myself.

拉康的第二幅图式如下一页所示，其中包含两层意思。第一层意思是，镜子的边界或边缘，最终成为了我们生活中每一位邻人、每一个想象的小他者的边缘与界限。这个作为镜像的小他者遮蔽了他者性的深渊，因此，邻人既是我自己的镜像，又并非我自己的镜像。

This second proposition is that the real object a, which is forever lost, dwells on the rim of the orifice of the imaginary body i’(a). This is because the disappearance of the real jouissance leaves investments in the erogenous zones and in the body’s rims. The rest of the object a will be left to reappear in the fantasy. Only under these conditions does the semblable/neighbor represent the secondary narcissism of everyone.

第二个命题指出，永远失落的实在的对象a，栖居于想象身体i’(a)的孔洞边缘。这是因为，实在享乐的消逝在动情区和身体边缘留下了投注。对象a的剩余部分，则会留待在幻象中重新显现。唯有在此条件下，相似者或邻人才能体现出每个人的次级自恋。

note：这里其实在说那个对象a作为塞子的图示，无意识作为一个幻想的空间，是被欲望客体a给堵住的，若没有a，无意识的言谈就会从中掉落出来。

This operation is not achieved fully in narcissistic neurosis. From this lost jouissance what is left is the phallicization of the body, achieved by the impact of the Other’s desire, directed in its name to the subject. If some leftover of this operation remains in the ego, it hurts there.

What would be discursive proof that this loss, in essence, took place? The relation to ourselves is made out of believing and not believing in ourselves. The radical suppression of the second term (not believing) leads to paranoia, and suppression of the first (believing) to melancholy. These two poor outcomes leave open or closed the first identification to primary narcissism.

From here stems the interpretative or depressive coloration of all remainders of primary narcissism in every subject. The clinical problem appears when this affective chromatism taints the entire life of the analysand, appealing before the symptoms as formations of the unconscious, even when no episode would make us think that we are facing a pre-psychotic state.

这一操作在自恋型神经症中并未彻底完成。从那失落的享乐中，留存下来的只有身体的菲勒斯化，这是大他者欲望冲击的结果，这欲望以大他者之名指向主体。要是这一操作还有什么残余留在自我里，就会在那里引发痛楚。

有什么话语层面的证据能证明这种失落本质上已经发生了呢？我们与自身的关系，是由相信自己和不相信自己构成的。彻底压抑后者（不相信自己），会导致偏执狂；彻底压抑前者（相信自己），则会导致忧郁。这两种不良后果，使得对原初自恋的首次认同要么处于敞开状态，要么处于封闭状态。

每个主体身上原初自恋的所有残余，都带有源于此的诠释或抑郁色彩。当这种情感色调浸染了分析者的整个生活，在症状中作为无意识形成物显现出来时，临床问题便随之出现，即便没有任何发作迹象让我们觉得面对的是前精神病状态。

note：这里叙述了两种精神病的结构，偏执狂和忧郁症。

There is first unity of the body i(a), a unity that the neurophysiologic body lacks and that is achieved by the care of the Other. First, the future subject exists as named; the Other addresses him, talks to him, and interprets the chance-movements of the real body as spoken replies. This unity is not sufficient to secure the structure and to protect the body from fragmentation in its confrontation with the Other’s desire.

首先，身体i(a)呈现出一种统一性，这是神经生理层面的身体所不具备的，它得益于大他者的照料才得以实现。起初，未来的主体以被命名的方式存在；大他者呼唤他、与他交谈，并将实在的身体的偶然动作解读为言语的回应。然而，这种统一性不足以稳固结构，也无法在身体直面大他者的欲望时，保护其免于支离破碎。

In this third schema, the letter A symbolizes the real Other as such, and the effect produced by the care and offer of love. They make the brain cortex work as an aspheric mirror, giving erogenous unity to the fragmented neurophysiologic body, a*1, making the entire body into an erogenous zone. This is what creates the first ego i(a). The most important aspect of the care is loving speech; it configures the first object of jouissance, a*2, around which the first imaginary body closes up. The letter represents the primordial identification, only possible if the Other desires, giving his lack to the subject as such.

在这第三个图式中，字母A象征着实在的大他者本身，也象征着照料与爱的给予所产生的效果。它们让大脑皮层像非球面镜那样运作，赋予碎片化的神经生理身体一种动情带的统一性，也就是a*1，从而把整个身体变成一个动情带。正是由此，第一个自我i(a)得以产生。照料中最关键的一环是爱的言语；它塑造了第一个享乐对象a*2，而第一个想象的身体正是围绕着它闭合起来的。这个字母代表着原初认同，而只有当大他者产生欲望，并将其匮乏给予主体本身时，这种认同才有可能发生。

The plane mirror represents the symbolic acquisition produced by the identification to a master signifier, S1, that is separated from the others and functions as an ideal. The plane mirror makes it possible for the object of jouissance in the real space, to the left of the schema, to disappear in the virtual space to the right. What does this mean? The breast as an oral object can never become a scopic object. This is to say that the two-year-old child can look at and envy the newborn at the breast, but can no longer remember the experience of feeling the breast as a part of his body. For the body to acquire its scopic status something new takes place—a loss. The imaginary character of the neighbor (no longer real) i’(a) offers the structural root for the potential to displace it, even if this loss is painful, as Freud proposed. What is painful is that, to access the status of neighbor, the other, any other, has to have for me a remainder of lost jouissance (a).

平面镜代表了一种象征性获取，它产生于对主能指S1的认同，这个主能指与其他能指相分离，并作为理想发挥作用。平面镜使得实在空间（即图式左侧）中的享乐对象，能够消失在右侧的虚拟空间中。这意味着什么？作为口腔对象的乳房，永远无法成为视觉对象。也就是说，两岁的孩子可以看着并嫉妒正在吃奶的新生儿，却再也无法记起将乳房感受为自己身体一部分的体验。身体要获得其视觉地位，就必须发生某种新的事情——一种丧失。邻人的想象性特质（不再是实在的）i’(a) 为置换这种丧失提供了结构性根源，即便正如弗洛伊德所指出的，这种丧失是痛苦的。痛苦之处在于，为了通达邻人的地位，他者，任何他者，都必须对我而言带有一种丧失的享乐(a)的残余。

note：看这个动作的发生，本身就建立在距离和分离之上。

Let us return to what we call narcissistic neurosis. What does Freud mean by non-sexual trauma when discussing war neurosis? Why did Lacan highlight the Freudian notion of Liebesversagung (love refusal) in those terms? Was Lacan referring to someone who as a child had been mistreated, humiliated, neglected and deprived of any protection? Lacan showed that the issue was not a lesser affective quantity but a loss of symbolic reference. It was in this sense that Freud had coined the concept non-sexual trauma, bringing it closer to the field of neurosis. Freud writes, “‘narcissistic libido,’ that is to say, a mass of sexual energy that attaches itself to the ego and satisfies itself with this as otherwise it does only with an object” (“«narzisstischen Libido», d.h. eines Masses von sexueller Energie, welches am Ich selbst hängt und sich an diesem ersättigt, wie sonst nur am Objekt”) (Freud 1999: 3, modified). Freud talks here of a modification (Veränderung) of the ego, which is not the modification described in Mourning and Melancholia, even though both texts were written at about the same time. There is a sexual object in the ego itself, and the ego is treated as an object not by the ideal, but by the libido that finds its satisfaction in it. What would this object in the ego be but the subject itself when invested as an object by an Other from which the subject is not separated, by an Other that continues investing the subject as an object of its own narcissism? At the same time, this object with which the subject identifies stands for the father or the mother, for those who maintained this type of relation with the subject.

让我们回到所谓的自恋型神经症。弗洛伊德在讨论战争神经症时，所说的“非性创伤”究竟指什么？拉康为何要用这些表述来强调弗洛伊德的“爱的拒绝”这一概念？拉康指的是一个在童年受尽虐待、羞辱、忽视且毫无保护的人吗？拉康指出，问题不在于情感量的减少，而在于象征参照的丧失。正是在这个意义上，弗洛伊德提出了“非性创伤”的概念，将其拉入了神经症的领域。弗洛伊德写道：“‘自恋力比多’，即一团附着在自我上并从中获得满足的性能量，正如它通常只在对象上获得满足一样。”弗洛伊德在此谈到自我的一种改变，这不同于《哀悼与忧郁》中描述的那种改变，尽管两篇文章写于大约同一时期。自我内部存在一个性对象，而自我被当作对象来对待，并非是被理想，而是被在其中获得满足的力比多。自我中的这个对象，除了主体自身还能是什么呢？此时主体被一个尚未与之分离的大他者投注为对象，被这个大他者持续当作自身自恋的对象来投注。同时，主体与之认同的这个对象代表着父亲或母亲，代表着那些与主体维持此种关系的人。

The subject’s problems with narcissism are the problems of the narcissism of the adult who represents the Other; they establish that the subject will be always both opposed to and completely sustained by that adult. Lacan mentions this inclusion of an object in the ego, thus opening not only a clinical issue of the Real, but also a theoretical problem that goes beyond the resolution of neurosis through the paternal metaphor. This metaphor does not fit with the theorization of what at that time had a double relation to castration. Lacan states:

Effectively, it is insofar as the castration complex is both crossed, but without being fully realized by the subject that there is an identification with a sort of raw image of the father, an image that carries a reflection of his real particularities in what they have as heavy, even crushing. Here we see once more a renewal of the mechanism of the reappearance in the Real, but this time a Real at the limit of the psychic, within the boundaries of the ego—of a real that imposes itself to the subject, in a quasi hallucinatory way, insofar as this subject, at some point, abandons the symbolic integration of the process of castration.

主体在自恋方面的问题，其实就是代表大他者的那个成人的自恋问题；这确立了主体与这个成人之间始终既对抗又完全受其支撑的关系。拉康提到自我对某个对象的纳入，这不仅引出了一个关于实在界的临床问题，也带来了一个理论问题，它超出了通过父亲隐喻解决神经症的范畴。这一隐喻无法契合当时关于阉割双重关系的理论建构。拉康指出：

实际上，正是因为主体遭遇了阉割情结，却未能将其彻底实现，才会去认同某种生硬的父亲意象，这个意象折射出父亲真实的特殊性，显得沉重甚至极具压迫感。在这里，我们再次看到实在界中重现机制的更新，但这一次，实在界处于心理的极限，在自我的边界之内——这是一种强加于主体的实在，以近乎幻觉的方式显现，只因主体在某个时刻放弃了对阉割过程的象征性整合。

note：这部分有点像之前讲的忧郁症那篇。

This is a singular and rich passage. In this seminar, Lacan comments on the case of Freud’s Little Hans, but here, without further ado, he launches into a long explanation about a different subject, who is not exactly Hans. We can hypothesize that he suddenly remembered the Wolf Man. He refers to a hallucination, like that of the cut finger hallucinated by the Wolf Man. Lacan remarks that what is at stake is not a return of something foreclosed in the Real, which rules out any diagnosis of psychosis. Lacan leaves open the issue of an ego, which includes an impassable Real, since the Real imposes itself. In Lacan’s thinking in the 1970s, this was schematized as an imaginary cord, or string, failed in the place where it goes above the Real in the Borromean knot. Lacan never spoke of this error in the knot,4 but I am inviting us to revisit it, especially in the neurotic structures, where a splinter of the Real never leaves the ego in peace. It satisfies itself without allowing us the simpler solution of assuming that this Real is just a remainder of jouissance asking for an encore.

这段文字独特且内涵丰富。在这次研讨班中，拉康点评了弗洛伊德的小汉斯案例，但在这里，他开门见山地长篇大论起另一个主体，一个不完全是汉斯的主体。我们可以假设，他突然想起了狼人。他提到了一种幻觉，就像狼人幻觉到自己手指被切断那样。拉康指出，这里的关键并非实在界中被除权之物的回归，这就排除了任何精神病的诊断。对于自我问题，拉康保持了开放态度，这个自我包含了一个不可逾越的实在界，因为实在界总会强加自身。在拉康70年代的思想中，这被图示化为博罗梅奥结中跨越实在界上方时断裂的一根想象界绳索。拉康从未谈论过结中的这种失误，但我邀请大家重新审视它，尤其是在神经症结构中，实在界的一个碎片永远不让自我安宁。它自我满足，而不允许我们采取一种更简单的解释，即假设这种实在界仅仅是要求“再来一次”的享乐残余。

The jouissance at play here is precisely the jouissance with which the subject struggles in narcissistic neurosis. It exercises its effects on the unconscious chain. This type of jouissance interferes with the creation of signifiers via metonymy and metaphor, thus impoverishing the investment of memories. It attacks the formation of dreams and parapraxes, and even symptom formations, because the subject cannot even articulate them as such. It is not what Lacan calls parasitic jouissance, but an opaque jouissance, useless for desire, love, and sublimation. Only a great poet such as Rainer Maria Rilke was able to transform this jouissance into something else, since he managed to testify to it by turning it into poetic work. Rilke made letters with it, used it as a lever and point of support for a possible love life. He consented to it, as a dimension from which, as a man, he had always been excluded, and that he had excluded himself from, because he considered it incompatible both with speech and writing. This is exactly what Lacan called “savoir y faire,” to be able to manage things.5

这里起作用的享乐，正是主体在自恋型神经症中苦苦挣扎的那种享乐。它在无意识链条上发挥着效应。这种享乐干扰了通过换喻和隐喻创造能指的过程，从而让对记忆的投注变得贫乏。它破坏了梦和过失行为的形成，甚至破坏了症状的形成，因为主体根本没法把它们作为症状表述出来。这不是拉康所说的寄生性享乐，而是一种不透明的享乐，对欲望、爱和升华毫无用处。只有像里尔克这样的大诗人，才能把这种享乐转化成别的东西，因为他成功地将其化作诗歌创作，从而为它作证。里尔克用它来写信，把它当成杠杆和支点，去支撑一种可能的爱情生活。他接纳了它，将其视为一个维度；身为男人，他一直把这个维度排斥在外，也把自己从中排斥出去，因为他觉得它跟言说和书写都不相容。这正是拉康所说的“savoir y faire”，也就是懂得如何应对。

note：原乐。

Freud tells a story in a short, yet famous essay, “Vergänglichkeit” (“On Transience”), of taking a walk through a landscape in full summer bloom in the company of a taciturn friend (Lou Andreas) and a young and already well known poet, a transparent allusion to Rilke: “The poet admired the beauty of the scene around us but felt no joy in it. He was disturbed by the thought that all this beauty was fated to extinction, that it would vanish when winter came, like all human beauty and all the beauty and splendor that men have created or may create. All that he would otherwise have loved and admired seemed to him to be shorn of its worth by the transience which was its doom (Schicksal der Vergänglichkeit)” (Freud 1982: 305).

弗洛伊德在一篇短小却著名的文章《无常》中讲过一段轶事：盛夏时节，万物繁茂，他与一位沉默寡言的朋友（露·安德烈亚斯）以及一位年轻且已声名鹊起的诗人一同漫步，这位诗人显然是在暗指里尔克：“诗人赞叹周遭景色的美丽，却感受不到丝毫喜悦。想到这一切美景注定要消亡，想到冬日一来它们便会消逝，正如人类所有的美，以及人类已创造或可能创造的一切美与辉煌都将灰飞烟灭，他便深感不安。那些他本该热爱与赞叹的事物，似乎都因这注定走向毁灭的无常而丧失了价值。”

Freud responded: “But this demand for immortality (Ewigkeitsforderung) is a product of our wishes (Wunschlebens) too unmistakable to lay claim to reality: what is painful may none the less be true. […] But I did dispute the pessimistic poet’s view that the transience (Vergänglichkeit) of what is beautiful involves any loss in its worth. […] On the contrary, an increase!” Freud continues:

What spoilt their enjoyment (Genuß) of beauty must have been a revolt in their minds against mourning. The idea that all this beauty was transient was giving these two sensitive minds a foretaste of mourning over its decease; and, since the mind instinctively recoils from anything that is painful, they felt their enjoyment of beauty interfered with by thoughts of its transience. […] I believe that those who think thus, and seem ready to make a permanent renunciation because what was precious has proved not to be lasting, are simply in a state of mourning for what is lost. […] Mourning, as we know, however painful it may be, comes to a spontaneous end. When it has renounced everything that has been lost, then it has consumed [aufgezehrt] itself, and our libido is once more free […].”

弗洛伊德回应道：“但这种对不朽的诉求，显然是我们愿望的产物，根本无法自诩为现实：令人痛苦的东西未必就不是真相。[…] 但我确实反驳了那位悲观诗人的看法，他认为美的事物的无常会折损其价值。[…] 恰恰相反，这反而增加了它的价值！”弗洛伊德接着说：

“破坏他们对美的享乐的，必定是内心对哀悼的抗拒。一想到这一切美都是无常的，就让这两个敏感的心灵提前尝到了哀悼其消亡的滋味；而且，由于心灵本能地回避任何痛苦的事物，他们便觉得对美的享乐被无常的念头干扰了。[…] 我认为，那些这样想、并且似乎准备因为珍贵之物无法长久而彻底放弃的人，仅仅是陷入了对失去之物的哀悼状态。[…] 我们知道，哀悼无论多么痛苦，都会自发地结束。当它放弃了一切失去的东西，它也就消耗殆尽了，而我们的力比多便再次获得自由[…]。”

He was spelling out his canonical theory of mourning: mourning consumes the subject when renouncing the lost object. Logically, Freud, nolens volens, allows us to see it, at the same time, as a condition of impossibility: one cannot renounce something lost when what is lost was never possessed. Two years later, under different circumstances, this will designate a particular modality of trauma, whose cause is a (theoretically) novel modality of refusal and failure.

他详细阐述了自己经典的哀悼理论：主体在放弃失去的客体时，会被哀悼所耗尽。从逻辑上看，弗洛伊德有意无意地让我们同时将其视为一种不可能性的条件：如果失去的东西从未被真正拥有，人就无从放弃它。两年后，在不同的情境下，这被用来指代一种特定的创伤样态，其起因是一种理论上全新的拒绝与失败样态。

Freud thus attributes to narcissistic libido a character that we can rethink through Lacan. Freud could not define it without renouncing his own attack on Jung, who clearly held that narcissistic libido was desexualized. Lacan introduces the Other’s jouissance as not having acceded to the subject the status of signifier or object of the fantasy. Rilke confirms this insight in one of his last poems, “Unfinished Elegy”:

因此，弗洛伊德赋予了自恋力比多某种特质，而我们可以借由拉康来重新思考这一特质。弗洛伊德若要界定它，就不得不放弃他对荣格的批判，因为荣格明确主张自恋力比多是去性化的。拉康引入了大他者的享乐，指出它并未赋予主体以能指或幻想对象的地位。里尔克在其晚期诗作《未完成的哀歌》中印证了这一洞见：

Don’t allow childhood, that nameless compact
with the heavenly, to be repudiated by destiny;
the prisoner himself broken in the dry prison
still has it to nurse him secretly to the end. Timelessly
it holds his heart. The diseased one too,
when he stares out and comprehends that even the room no more
offers an answer, because it can yet be healed—healable also
his objects lie about among his losses, feverish and diseased,
but still healable—: for him
childhood yet bears fruit. Purely
amid decaying nature it tends its welcoming bed
Not that it is innocent; its prettied up deceptions,
its bed-shams and bolsters, only fleetingly delude us.
Never more certain than we, never more naïve.
Nothing godly can weigh against its weight.
Defenseless just like we are; like animals in winter are, defenseless.
But more so because childhood has no hiding places.
Defenseless as if itself were the threat.
Defenseless like fire, like a giant, like poison, like goings-on
behind locked doors at night in a suspicious house.
For those who fail to see that the hands of the home
Lie, those that protect—are themselves in danger. Who has the right then?

别让童年，
那份与神圣大他者缔结的无名契约，被命运除权；
即便是身陷干涸牢笼、被彻底击碎的囚徒，也仍有它在暗中哺育自己，直到终点。
它超越时间，紧握着他的心。
还有那患病者，当他向外凝视，意识到连这房间也不再给出任何回应，因为大他者已无法给出答案——而他的那些对象散落在他的缺失之中，狂热而病态，却依然可被治愈——：
对他而言，童年依然结出果实。在衰败的自然中，它纯粹地照料着那张迎候的床。
并非说它是无辜的；它那粉饰过的假相，那些床帏与靠枕的伪装，只能短暂地蒙蔽我们。
它从不比我们更确定，也从不比我们更天真。没有任何神圣大他者的分量能与之抗衡。
它毫无防备，就像我们一样；就像冬日的野兽，毫无防备。但更是如此，因为童年没有藏身之处。
它毫无防备，仿佛它本身就是那个威胁。它毫无防备，如同烈火，如同巨人，如同毒药，如同深夜可疑房屋里紧锁的门后发生的勾当。
因为那些看不透“家”的双手在撒谎的人，那些以为受到保护的人——他们自己正身处险境。那么，谁又有权呢？

This poem, written four years after that summer walk with Freud, seems not just to answer but to agree with Freud’s interpretation. Rilke clarifies here the nature of mourning that he could not complete earlier. This poem exhibits a tendency we find in many patients, which can be called a wish to be death’s neighbor. Here we find not the suspicious collision with death that aims at obtaining secondary gains, but a state of endless mourning. Proximity to death can obtain a surplus jouissance, the extra jouissance with which death has been invested. In Rilke’s case, this manifests itself in the attempt to approach death in writing. The erogenous temptation of death appears meaningful in a world that has been annihilated. There is still a hope of separating from the first Other, of tearing oneself from it, a hope comparable to the dangerous belief that the act of killing oneself would make someone be born again.

这首诗写于与弗洛伊德那次夏日散步的四年之后，似乎不仅是在回应弗洛伊德的阐释，更是在赞同其观点。里尔克在此澄清了他早年未能完成的哀悼的本质。这首诗展现出我们在许多患者身上都能看到的一种倾向，可以称之为渴望成为死亡的邻居。在这里，我们看到的不是为了获取继发性获益而与死亡发生的可疑碰撞，而是一种无尽的哀悼状态。靠近死亡能够获取一种剩余享乐，即死亡被投注的那份额外的享乐。在里尔克这里，这表现为试图在书写中靠近死亡。在一个已被彻底摧毁的世界里，死亡的动情区诱惑显得意味深长。这里依然残存着一种与原初大他者分离、将自己从中撕裂出来的希望，这种希望堪比一种危险的信念，即自杀之举能让某人获得重生。

The work of a great poet sheds some light on the attention we should pay to what gets written in the analysis of each analysand, as an effect of speech, which makes each cure a sort of letter that becomes readable. This is a letter that did not exist before the analytic work, and such a letter comes to find its proper place between the voice and the gaze. The wish to remain, at any cost, in the proximity of death allows us to see something that was not fully accomplished in the first dialectic of alienation and separation. This is what Lacan has described as the “lethal factor” (Lacan 1981: 208, 213), a point of death that marks the jouissance of life. If it is not spoken, at least it is written. “Lethal”6 is the forgetting of one’s previous existence after one has crossed the waters of the Lethe. It is the jug from which the souls of the dead drink when arriving to Averno. It is the forgetting of forgetting, and here it acquires a positive value. Lethal is what is hidden forever from that period of life, or the original repression, Urverdrängung.

一位伟大诗人的作品启发我们去关注，在每位分析者的分析中，作为言语的效应，究竟有什么被书写了下来，这使得每一次治疗都化作一种可供阅读的文字。这种文字在分析工作开始前并不存在，它恰恰在声音与凝视之间找到了自己恰当的位置。不惜一切代价也要靠近死亡的愿望，让我们看到了在异化与分离的第一重辩证法中未能彻底完成的东西。这就是拉康所说的“致死因素”，一个标记着生命享乐的死亡之点。如果它未被言说，至少它已被书写。“致死”指的是渡过勒忒河后对前世存在的遗忘。它是亡魂抵达阿维尔诺时用来饮水的水罐。它是对遗忘的遗忘，并由此获得了一种积极的价值。“致死”之物，是那段生命历程中被永远隐藏起来的东西，即原初压抑。

The narcissistic subjects we treat have not been blessed by that water of oblivion and by its lethal effect that create an incorporeal space7 for words. It allows a satisfaction in speech without thinking that speech always carries consequences. Those not blessed by Lethe will carry the task of deciding not just to keep on living their lives, but to sustain life with a saying that belongs to them alone. They suffer from an excessive speech that is not free from jouissance, and at the same time from a speech with a jouissance that was scarce. We see this theme in one of the Sonnets to Orpheus, written in 1922 as a funerary monument in the Castle of Muzot:

Who, with the dead, on poppy dine,
need nevermore fear:
the true sweetness in his rhyme
shall never disappear.

我们治疗的自恋主体，未曾蒙受忘川之水的赐福，也未领受其致命效果，而正是这效果为词语辟出了一片无形空间。它让人在言说中获得满足，而不必担忧言说总会带来后果。那些未受忘川赐福的人，必须承担起一项抉择的重任：不仅要继续生活，还要用独属于自己的言说来支撑生命。他们饱受双重折磨：一方面是过度且裹挟着享乐的言说，另一方面则是享乐匮乏的言说。我们在1922年写于穆佐城堡、作为墓志铭的《致俄耳甫斯的十四行诗》中看到了这一主题：

与死者同食罂粟者，
自此无所畏惧：
其诗韵中的真甘美，
永远不会消逝。

Rilke’s poetry, like any great art, asks without idealization what sublimation means in an analysis. Sublimation is a work on jouissance but, as defined by Freud, it is zielgehemmt, i.e. inhibited as to its sexual goal, which strictly means that it loses it aptitude for reaching orgasm. This does not mean that it would not maintain its nature of jouissance in regards to its sexual aim, with all the inherent difficulties implied by jouissance. It may cause anxiety, inhibitions, and symptomatic formations, as a jouissance not inhibited in terms of its objective would. An object that is also an instrument provides this jouissance, as Real. This is much more attractive for a great artist, at least an artist who engages in a deadly struggle with words, sounds, or colors, who finds that mere reality is greyer and far more secondary than what common mortals think. In general, this requires a share of pain that readers or spectators will want to pay to ignore.

里尔克的诗歌，如同一切伟大的艺术，在不加理想化的前提下，追问升华在分析中究竟意味着什么。升华是对享乐的一种加工，但正如弗洛伊德所界定的，它是“目标受抑”的，即其性目标受到抑制，严格来说就是丧失了达到高潮的能力。但这并不意味着，就其性目的而言，它不再保持享乐的本性，也不意味着它摆脱了享乐固有的种种难题。它可能会引发焦虑、抑制和症状形成，就像那些目标未受抑制的享乐一样。一个兼具工具属性的对象，作为实在界，提供了这种享乐。这对伟大的艺术家来说要有吸引力得多，至少对那些与词语、声音或色彩殊死搏斗的艺术家而言是如此，在他们眼中，单纯的现实远比凡夫俗子以为的更加灰暗、更加次要。通常，这需要承受一份痛苦，而读者或观众往往宁愿付出代价去对其视而不见。

Sublimation does not mean de-sexualization; on the contrary, it is the raising of an object to the dignity of the Thing, thus providing a jouissance thanks to the words used by the poet when writing. These are not strictly words from ordinary language but those that the poet has extracted from it, so as to mold them anew. This task is as dangerous as that of the mountain climber who risks death, reaching summits by way of the steepest paths, like the explorer who defies lands never traversed to leave on them, and in the memory of everyone, the trace of his passing.

升华绝不意味着去性化；恰恰相反，它是将对象提升至原质的尊严，从而借由诗人落笔时的词句带来一种享乐。这些词严格来说并非日常用语，而是诗人从中萃取出来、重新打磨的字句。这番劳作凶险万分，就像登山者冒着生命危险，沿着最陡峭的险径攀登绝顶，又似探险家无畏踏入无人涉足的荒野，只为在那片土地和众人的记忆中留下自己走过的印记。

An artist is not someone who manages to give shape in writing to his own fantasies, as Freud seems to imply in The Poet and Daydreaming. An artist is someone who, because of a failure in fantasy, uses writing to relate to others, which allows him to have a semblance, while at the same time not being himself. A great poet is his work; it is the trace of his existence. Thanks to his work, he maintains himself in the despair of failing to say something that before it was written he did not know.

艺术家并不是那种能在写作中成功为自己的幻想赋形的人，尽管弗洛伊德在《诗人与白日梦》里似乎暗示了这一点。艺术家其实是这样的人：正因为幻想的失败，他借由写作与他人建立联系，这让他得以披上一层假相，同时又无需充当他自己。伟大的诗人即其作品，那是他存在的痕迹。正是凭借作品，他让自己持存于这样一种绝望之中：他无法言说某物，而这某物在落笔之前他根本无从知晓。

There is analytic work to be done here which does not primarily concern the formations of the unconscious. When patients feel that they have to be “unique” but, not psychotic, they have not been subjected to foreclosure, such patients strive for a signifier of the One by which they hope to obtain human stature. If not for this desperate attempt, they would be reduced to refuse. Only by fulfilling that improbable task will they return to the “one amongst others” that defines human society. They will be redeemed from moral fault, whether the fault be one really committed or simply attributed to their parents. Once more, Rilke expresses this well:

Who, if I cried out, would hear me among the Angelic
Orders? And even if one were to suddenly
take me to its heart, I would vanish into its
stronger existence. For beauty is nothing but
the beginning of terror.

这里有一项分析工作要做，它主要关涉的并非无意识形成物。当患者觉得自己必须“独一无二”，却又并非精神病，即未曾遭遇除权时，他们便会苦苦追寻“一”的能指，指望借此获得人的身位。若非这番绝望的挣扎，他们只会沦为一堆废料。唯有完成这项近乎不可能的任务，他们才能回归那种定义人类社会的“众人中的一员”。他们将从道德罪疚中解脱，无论这罪疚是自己真切犯下的，还是仅仅被归咎于父母的。里尔克再次绝妙地道出了这种心境：

若我呼喊，天使的序列中
有谁听得见我？即便有谁
猛然将我拥入怀中，我也会消融于他
更强大的存在。因为美无非是
恐怖的开端。

What is this terror? Freud’s metaphor, referring to narcissistic neurosis as war neurosis, is more profound; he suggests that these analysands live in a state of war, and that such a belief can be a weapon against the precariousness that was the previous norm. If it is true that the death drive is found when drives unravel themselves, then a last quatrain, written in French by Rilke, can show how this unraveling has become a creative instrument:

Il faut fermer les yeux et renoncer à la bouche
Rester muet, aveugle, ébloui
L’espace tout ébranlé qui nous touche
Ne veut de notre être que l’ouïe.
[One has to close one’s mouth and eyes
Stay mute and blinded by light rays,
Space a trembling around us all touching
Only wants of us we keep hearing.]

这究竟是怎样的恐惧？弗洛伊德的隐喻更为深刻，他将自恋神经症比作战争神经症，暗示这些分析者正生活在一种战争状态中，并指出这种信念能化作武器，去对抗以往那种岌岌可危的常态。如果说死亡冲动确实在冲动自行解体时显现，那么里尔克用法语写下的最后一首四行诗，便能展现这种解体如何化作一种创造性的工具：

必须闭上双眼，舍弃双唇
保持缄默，盲目，目眩神迷
那震颤着将我们触及的浩瀚空间
对我们的存在别无所求，唯求聆听。

In this poem, Rilke confesses his will to lose all senses for the benefit of only one, hearing. The jouissance of an isolated drive is superior to that of entangled drives. Writing is an instrument of re-enmeshing—after having enjoyed the creation of metaphors, the gift of only one drive is accepted in silence because the pen is also a sword filled with an ink replacing blood. Then the poet gathers the invocatory drive with the other senses, making life possible. This is how Rilke finally answers Freud, beyond the death of both. Mistrust in transience, in the perishable beauty accessible to the senses, is justified and true, and indeed it allows the poet to recreate the Other as a poem. In the poem, there are no seasons in which the flowers wither; the written words know nothing of spring, neither do they know of icy winters, since this is the constant temperature of the heart. This is why there is always a fear of abandoning pain, as if pain was more real, for any distancing of pain will immediately produce a feeling of unreality.

在这首诗中，里尔克坦白了自己的心愿：为了成全唯一的听觉，他宁愿丧失所有其他感官。孤立冲动的享乐胜过绞缠冲动的享乐。书写是一种重新绞缠的工具——在享受了创造隐喻的过程后，唯一冲动的馈赠在沉默中被接纳，因为笔也是一把剑，充盈着替代血液的墨水。随后，诗人将召唤冲动与其他感官汇聚，使生命成为可能。里尔克正是以此最终回应了弗洛伊德，超越了二者的死亡。对无常、对感官可及的易逝之美抱有不信任，是正当且真实的，这确实让诗人得以将大他者重塑为一首诗。在诗中，没有花朵凋零的季节；写下的文字不知春为何物，也不识凛冬，因为这是心灵恒定的温度。正因如此，人总是害怕抛弃痛苦，仿佛痛苦更为真实，因为任何对痛苦的疏离都会立刻引发一种非现实感。

But the Other born with the poem disappears once it is written. What does not vanish, however, is the absolutely singular use of the words that say something that nobody else has ever said, and that nobody will say. In this we see the invention of the Real. Writing replaces a speech that never, or almost never, carried a saying. It luminously recreates so much that it is blinding, for here is the other Φ, not the undernourished or perishing desire of the Other. This poetic Real puts order in what is offered as a signifier for life. Maybe a woman, Baladine Klossowska, who was already the mother of the future Balthus and of Pierre, fulfilled for some time the role of what a woman can be for a man: Merline, she was as he named her.

但是，伴随诗歌诞生的大他者，一旦写成便消隐无踪。然而不会消散的，是那种绝对独特的词语用法，它言说出从未有人说过、也再不会有人说的东西。在此，我们看到了实在界的创生。书写取代了那种从未或几乎从未承载过言说的言语。它的重构如此光芒四射，以至于令人目眩，因为这里显现的是另一个Φ，而非大他者那贫乏或濒死的欲望。这种诗意的实在界，为那些充当生命能指的东西理出了秩序。也许，巴拉迪娜·克洛索夫斯卡这个女人，作为未来的巴尔蒂斯和皮埃尔的母亲，曾在一段时间里充当了一个女人对男人而言所能是的那个角色：他叫她梅林娜。

When we listen to some analysands, the impression they leave us is not so much an unfinished mourning as a lack of jouissance in the relation with the other, or of a jouissance that was badly directed. One may imagine that the little bit of jouissance is like the flowers from the first mirror that disappear in the second mirror. Those flowers may have been lacking in sun or in sap, since they are barely sketched, an opaque background of said words, or perhaps even this thought is nothing but a retrospective optical illusion. The question remains: how can we mourn what never was?

当我们倾听某些分析者时，他们留下的印象与其说是未完成的哀悼，不如说是在与小他者的关系中缺乏享乐，或者是享乐的方向出了偏差。我们不妨想象，那一丁点享乐就像第一面镜子里的花，在第二面镜子里却消失不见了。那些花或许是缺了阳光或汁液，因为它们只是勉强勾勒出轮廓，成了所说话语的不透明背景；又或者，这种想法本身不过是一种事后的光学错觉。问题依然存在：我们该如何哀悼那从未存在过的东西？

The work of Rilke as a poet (Dichter in German means creator) teaches us how human speech can find meaning again when the desire of the Other, which only existed intermittently as a contradiction, prevented the language of the mother from giving it beforehand. This entailed a path through writing as recreation of the mother tongue, giving to his words a meaning that they never had because they never were gathered in that way. The “infancy” of which he talks as poet is not even the originary helplessness in early life, but a childhood reinvented so as to receive from language words that did not mean anything. Those meaningless words made it impossible to have a body for oneself and for the others; he returns them written with a signification that makes them forever new, impossible to be worn out. Ultimately, Rilke teaches us that poetic saying passes through a letter that was not yet written.

里尔克作为诗人的作品（德语Dichter一词意为创造者）向我们揭示了，当大他者的欲望——它仅作为一种矛盾间歇性地存在——阻碍了母亲的语言预先赋予人类言语以意义时，人类言语如何能重新找回意义。这势必要求一条通过书写来重塑母语的道路，赋予他的词语一种它们从未有过的意义，因为它们从未以那样的方式被聚合过。他作为诗人所谈论的“幼年”，甚至不是生命早期的原初无助，而是一种被重新发明的童年，以便从语言中接收那些毫无意义的词语。那些无意义的词语使人无法为自己和他人拥有一具身体；他以书写的方式将它们归还，赋予其一种意指，使它们永远崭新，无法被耗尽。最终，里尔克教导我们，诗性言说必须穿过一个尚未被写下的字母。

note：这篇论文一般，主要搞得很晦涩又言之无物，还不如未弃置客体那篇，后面一直在讲圣状，我感觉不如直接用博洛米结解释下再引下乔伊斯的例子。

第十三章：她彻底疯了：癔症、女人与精神分析家（SHE’S RAVING MAD：The hysteric, the woman, and the psychoanalyst）——克洛德-诺埃尔·皮克曼（Claude-Noële Pickmann）

It is well-known that the hysteric led Freud to his discovery of the unconscious. In return, she made the psychoanalyst the privileged addressee of her question, and the enigma of femininity immediately took center stage in the development of psychoanalysis.

众所周知，正是癔症引导弗洛伊德发现了无意识。作为回报，她将精神分析家指定为自己提问的特权对象，而女性特质之谜也随即在精神分析的发展中占据了核心位置。

note：这个观点很有趣，癔症话语作为分析家话语的逆时针旋转，从逻辑顺序上是作为分析家的原因存在的，这里并非是分析家来“治疗”癔症，而是癔症指定分析家，反而突显出了癔症才是这个辩证法中真正的主人位置。

“What is a woman?”—That is the question for the hysteric. Looking for a response to this intractable enigma, she addresses her question to the father, pushing him unremittingly all the way to his jouissance; or just as well, by identifying herself with a man, she cross-examines man’s desire for a woman.

“女人是什么？”——这正是癔症的问题。为了回应这个棘手的谜题，她将问题抛向父亲，不断逼迫他，直至触及他的享乐；或者，她同样可以认同一个男人，反过来质询男人对女人的欲望。

note：这个问题也正是癔症话语中说明的，她们逼迫大他者回答问题，反而是在激发大他者的求知欲，最终结果是生产出了大他者的匮乏——对象a。

This question that the hysteric emblematically sustains is also the one at the heart of psychoanalytic knowledge. Indeed, the thesis that the unconscious is determined by the phallus, a fact that Freud highlighted in what he gleaned from his patients—and most especially from his hysterical patients’ speech—demonstrates that the feminine sex, as such, is excluded from the unconscious. While there are in fact two anatomical sexes, the unconscious does not recognize this biological reality. It neglects it, Freud concludes in his “Three Essays on the Theory of Sexuality” (1905), and recognizes the phallus as the only sexual symbol. Since the difference between the sexes is not inscribed in the unconscious, what is inscribed in its place is a failure in jouissance that Freud designates by the term castration, whose effects are different for boys and for girls.

癔症典型地维系的那个问题，也正是精神分析知识的核心问题。事实上，弗洛伊德从其病人——尤其是从癔症病人的言说中——所提炼出的一个关键论点是：无意识是由菲勒斯所决定的。这一事实表明，女性性别本身被排除在无意识之外。尽管解剖学上确实存在两种性别，但无意识并不承认这种生物学现实。弗洛伊德在《性学三论》（1905）中总结道，无意识对此视而不见，只认菲勒斯为唯一的性象征。既然性别差异并未铭刻于无意识之中，取而代之被铭刻下来的，便是一种享乐的失败——弗洛伊德称之为“阉割”，而其效应在男孩与女孩身上各不相同。

note：这种享乐（性完满）的失败，也正是性化的开始，只有享乐被屏蔽了，主体才能存活。

This is the scandal that Freud has been violently reproached for, namely, that he did not understand the feminine. His error was in wanting to explain the feminine by way of a conceptual tool that cannot be applied to it. Indeed, how can Freud successfully define the specificities of the feminine when he uses the theory of the primacy of the phallus and the logic of castration implied by it?

这正是弗洛伊德饱受激烈指责的丑闻：他未能理解女性特质。他的错误在于试图用一种根本无法适用于女性特质的概念工具去解释它。的确，当弗洛伊德援引菲勒斯优先的理论及其所蕴含的阉割逻辑时，他又怎能成功界定女性特质的独特性呢？

Jones and the British school criticized Freud already in the 1930s, opposing his phallocentrism, which they considered as theoretically inadequate with regard to women. They posited a “natural” or “primary” femininity that daughters would inherit directly from their mothers and that would orient both their feminine desire and their jouissance. This “natural” femininity was ascribed to anatomical determination, in contradiction with clinical evidence that men and women are both beings of language and that their relation to sex is subverted by this fact.

琼斯及其英国学派早在1930年代就已批评弗洛伊德，反对他所谓的菲勒斯中心主义，认为这种立场在理论上对女性而言是不充分的。他们提出一种“自然的”或“原初的”女性特质，认为女儿会直接从母亲那里继承这种特质，并由此定向其女性欲望与享乐。这种“自然的”女性特质被归因于解剖学决定论，但这与临床证据相矛盾——临床表明，无论男女，都是语言的存在，而他们与性的关系恰恰被这一事实所颠覆。

note：琼的概念是非常奇怪的，他所谓的这种女性特质难道不是一个假相吗？这里越想直接以一个无逻辑而必然的从母亲那边继承女性特质来叙说女性特质（本质主义），却愈发在肯定类似的荒诞的男性性别。

On the other hand, the Freudian thesis makes us aware that unconscious knowledge does not say it all and, notably, it says nothing about the female sex, which, as such, is excluded. Indeed, the unconscious is founded on this rejection. The irreducible phallocentrism of the unconscious is an obstacle to the male-female relation. This failure in jouissance inherent to human sexuality is related to the absence of complementarity or harmony between the sexes. We can thus summarize this major discovery of psychoanalysis: at the fundament of the world, the world that is ordered by phallic semblants, there are not two sexes; there is no primary complementarity,2 but only a non-rapport. This is the Real of sexuation3 that both men and women must confront in their encounter with the other sex.

另一方面，弗洛伊德的论点提醒我们：无意识的知识并非无所不包——尤其它对女性性别根本一言不发，而这种沉默本身正是一种排除。事实上，无意识正是建立在这种拒认之上。无意识中不可化约的菲勒斯中心主义，构成了男女之间关系的障碍。人类性欲中固有的享乐之失败，正源于两性之间缺乏互补性或和谐。因此，我们可以将精神分析这一重大发现概括如下：在世界的根基处——即那个由菲勒斯假相所秩序化的世界里——并不存在两种性别；不存在原初的互补性，而只有一种“没有性关系”。这便是性化的实在界，无论男性还是女性，在与异性相遇时都必须直面这一实在。

note：这里的无意识感觉可以直接转译为象征界。

S(Ⱥ)：拉康的情书（S(Ⱥ): Lacan’s love letter）

If woman, as such, does not exist in the unconscious, then we cannot find a distinctive feminine trait that would pertain to all women. Therefore, we must renounce the possibility of finding the essence of femininity. Lacan will summarize this fundamental axiom of the Freudian discovery in an aphorism that is at once provocative and concise: “Ŧħɇ woman does not exist” (Lacan 1998b: 7). He writes this in barring the definite article, “the.” What does not exist is the predicate that would accompany all women, the feminine universal, the identificatory trait that would determine what is a woman. Women, of course, exist! Consequently, each woman will have to invent her own solution to make up for this flaw in sexual identity, her own way of being a woman.

既然女人作为“女人”本身在无意识中并不存在，那么我们就无法找到一个适用于所有女性的独特女性特质。因此，我们必须放弃寻找女性特质之本质的可能。拉康将弗洛伊德这一根本性发现浓缩为一句既挑衅又简洁的格言：“女人不存在”（Lacan 1998b: 7）。他特意划去了定冠词“the”——不存在的，是那个能统摄所有女性的谓词，是那个女性的普遍性，是那个能界定“何为女人”的认同性特征。当然，女人是存在的！正因如此，每一位女性都必须自行发明属于自己的解决方案，以弥补这一性别身份中的裂隙，创造出自己做女人的方式。

note：女性不被定义的出处——女性无法被菲勒斯逻辑定义。

Due to this fact, what a woman encounters when confronted with castration is not only an unbearable inequality between the sexes. We know that women have always been able to make the best of being of the so-called “weaker sex,” but it is “the profound Werwerfung of the woman, her rejection as a being” (Lacan 1998a: 350) that is at stake. Lacan designates this emptiness of existence (for women)—the one that women face insofar as they are women—with the matheme, S(Ⱥ): the signifier of the barred Other, the “fault, hole, or loss in the Other” (Lacan 1998b: 28). This illustrates the dimension of inconsistency, that the Other qua locus “does not hold up.”5 In this sense, the S(Ⱥ), expresses that the Symbolic is “not-all.” Furthermore, it points to an irreducible Real, which is produced as an excess to every symbolization process. This Real cannot be written: the Other is definitively irreducible to the one of the identifiable,6 i.e., hétéros. Lacan’s thesis in “Encore”: “By S(Ⱥ) I designate nothing other than woman’s jouissance” (Lacan 1998b: 64). Feminine jouissance appears as heterogeneous to phallic jouissance; it is not its symmetrical feminine counterpart. Nevertheless, it is situatable in relation to phallic jouissance as an opening to another dimension. This is why Lacan called it supplementary jouissance. As such, feminine jouissance escapes the phallic signifier, producing an Other dimension, which is its own beyond, and which doesn’t allow any complementarity of the partner in the sexual relation.

正因如此，女性在面对阉割时所遭遇的，远不止是两性之间那种令人难以承受的不平等。我们知道，女性历来都能巧妙地利用所谓“弱势性别”的处境，但真正关键的是“女性深刻的Werwerfung，即她作为存在被拒斥”（拉康 1998a: 350）。拉康用数学型 S(Ⱥ) 来标示这种（专属于女性的）存在的空无——即女性就其为女性而言所直面的那种空无：这是被划杠的大他者的能指，意指“大他者中的裂隙、空洞或缺失”（拉康 1998b: 28）。这揭示了大他者作为场所的不一致性，即它“撑不住”。在这个意义上，S(Ⱥ) 表明象征界是“非全”的。此外，它还指向一种不可化约的实在界——这种实在界作为一切符号化过程的剩余而产生出来。这一实在界无法被书写：大他者从根本上无法被还原为可识别之“太一”，亦即“异质者”。正如拉康在《再来一次》中所提出的论点：“我用 S(Ⱥ) 所指的，无非就是女性的享乐”（拉康 1998b: 64）。女性享乐与菲勒斯享乐异质；它并非后者的对称性女性对应物。然而，它可以在与菲勒斯享乐的关系中被定位为通向另一维度的开口。正因如此，拉康称之为“增补性享乐”。就此而言，女性享乐逃逸于菲勒斯能指之外，生产出一个“他者维度”——那是它自身的彼岸，且不允许在性关系中存在任何伴侣间的互补性。

note：这里可能有一个勘误，Werwerfung->Verwerfung，翻译为拒斥。这句话：“By S(Ⱥ) I designate nothing other than woman’s jouissance”，实际上指涉了好几个层面：

  1. 女性集合是被大他者（象征界）排除的，也就是并非所有女性被阳具逻辑定义。
  2. 这种排除产生剩余对存在匮乏的大他者来说无法被象征化的。
  3. 菲勒斯享乐的局部性相比于女性享乐来说是非全的、部分的。
