Lacan designated this Other jouissance by the term “pas tout” (not-all),7 showing that the universality of the phallic order is already flawed. The “not-all” is a subjective position that is not limited to women, yet the way women encounter the castration of the Other produces a more immediate relation with the failure in the Other than it does in men. Indeed, a woman, in search of a referent to identify herself with the signifier “woman,” finds an absence instead of the signifier she had hoped for.8 The feminine position is thus revealed as divided with regard to the phallic function: this relation to the phallic function is only partial; it is not generalizable, and as such, it is undecidable. This is why one cannot confuse the feminine position with hysteria; hysteria is the position of the one who has decided not to play in this undecidability and consequently cannot enjoy it.

拉康用“非全”一词来指称这种大他者的享乐，表明菲勒斯秩序的普遍性本身已然存在裂隙。“非全”是一种主体位置，并不仅限于女性；然而，女性遭遇大他者之阉割的方式，使她们比男性更直接地触及大他者内部的失败。事实上，一个女人在寻找能让她认同“女人”这一能指的参照点时，所发现的却是一种缺席，而非她所期待的那个能指。因此，女性位置就其与菲勒斯功能的关系而言，呈现出一种分裂：这种关系只是局部的、不可普遍化的，因而也是不可判定的。正因如此，我们不能将女性位置与癔症混为一谈；癔症恰恰是那种拒绝进入这种不可判定性、因而也无法从中获得享乐的位置。

note：这里是除了“女性引发欲望，癔症引发求知欲”以外的另一个区分女性&癔症，即女性是找不到性别能指参照（毕竟最后发现都是假相）而发现了大他者内部的失败，而癔症是发现大他者内部失败以后拒绝了女性的位置——那么难道就是癔症的位置？

Following Freud and then Lacan, if an anti-phallic essence of femininity does not exist, that is to say, if the feminine exception does not exist, if there isn’t anybody or anything from the woman’s side that is able to found the existence of a non-phallic sex,9 how can a woman enjoy this part of herself where she is radically Other for herself?

继弗洛伊德之后，拉康指出：如果女性特质并无一种反菲勒斯的本质，也就是说，如果根本不存在所谓“女性的例外”；如果从女性这一侧，并没有任何人或任何东西能够确立一种非菲勒斯性别的存在，那么，一个女人又如何能享受她自身中那个对她自己而言彻底是大他者的部分呢？

note：说的有点绕，其实就是既然女性没有参照物或者说性别定义（不存在x不受Φ定义），一个女人又怎么去获得大他者享乐的。

为何是无而非有（Why is there nothing rather than something?）

If the phallic law that structures the world fails to make woman an “all” (complete) subject, what happens to the unsubjectifiable part of herself? Is it not this very question that the hysteric addresses to the father when she demonstrates the failure of the phallic order to account for the possible existence of a feminine being? She thus denounces the limits of the Symbolic order by questioning the structure at the very point of its failure. It is in this way that the hysteric has an almost natural vocation to make herself a symptom of the Master’s discourse, and to incarnate at each historic moment the symptomatic truth of the social malaise. Her symptom takes various forms in regard to this malaise in order to better point it out.

如果建构世界的菲勒斯法则无法将女人构成为“全体”（完整的）主体，那么她身上那无法被主体化的部分又会怎样？难道不正是这个问题，构成了癔症者向父亲提出的质询——当她揭示出菲勒斯秩序无力说明女性存在的可能性时？由此，她恰恰在象征秩序失效之处对其结构提出质疑，从而揭露出该秩序的界限。正是在这个意义上，癔症者几乎天然地注定要使自己成为主人话语的症状，并在每一个历史时刻化身社会不适的症状性真相。她的症状会以各种形式呈现，以便更清晰地指明这种社会不适。

The hysterical phallic gripe, her general complaint, demonstrates the failure of identification of woman with the phallus, showing that a type of jouissance exists beyond the phallus. The hysteric reminds us that the question of femininity can only be posed where the phallic function meets its limit, opening to an infinite beyond, that of an Other jouissance. The hysteric refuses to acknowledge that the phallus as semblant could serve jouissance. Her mistake consists in trying to demonstrate that because it escapes the phallus, it would not owe anything to it and tries to testify to a magnified and untouched “essence” of femininity. Recall Dora in ecstasy, “rapt in silent admiration” (Freud 1905a: 95) in front of the Dresden Madonna or the hypnotic ravishing of Lol V. Stein when the cause of desire appears in the form of Anne Marie Stretter whom she ideally recognizes as “The woman.”

癔症主体对菲勒斯的牢骚——她那普遍性的抱怨——揭示了女性无法与菲勒斯认同的失败，从而表明存在一种超越菲勒斯的享乐。癔症提醒我们：女性特质的问题，唯有在菲勒斯功能抵达其界限之处才能被提出，由此开启一个无限的彼岸，即大他者的享乐。癔症拒绝承认，作为假相的菲勒斯也能服务于享乐。她的错误在于试图证明：既然这种享乐逃逸于菲勒斯之外，便无需向菲勒斯有所亏欠；并力图见证一种被放大、未受玷污的女性“本质”。不妨回想多拉在德累斯顿圣母像前陷入狂喜，“沉浸于无声的赞叹之中”（Freud 1905a: 95）；又或洛尔·V·斯坦在欲望之因以安妮·玛丽·斯特雷特的形象显现时所经历的催眠式迷醉——她将后者理想化地认作“那个女人”。

note：癔症在面对菲勒斯功能在女性问题上的无能之时，试图去拜托菲勒斯功能而去追求另一个“菲勒斯”——孤立的女性特质，即和前文所说的琼的错误是一致的。

Correlatively, the hysteric has been and remains just as much an example of the fact that a girl, during the Oedipus complex, chooses the father and his desire as a privileged partner in order to pose the question of the woman. Dora and the young homosexual woman both showed Freud, even before he was able to fully understand it, that a girl learns to use the father or his desiring gaze; if these fail, she uses the signifiers of his desire in order to become a woman. This is due to the fact that in the relation to the father, lack becomes possible. This possibility offers her a way out; while she doesn’t have the phallus, she can still try to be it … for masculine desire. She will thus be able to play—at times with some pleasure—at being the phallus, experiencing the ravaging effects that a woman can obtain from making herself the object cause of desire for a man.

相应地，癔症患者过去是、现在依然是这样一个例证：女孩在俄狄浦斯情结期间，选择父亲及其欲望作为优先的搭档，以此提出“女人是什么”的问题。多拉和那位年轻的同性恋女性都向弗洛伊德表明了这一点——甚至在他尚不能完全理解之时——即女孩学会利用父亲本人，或其欲望的凝视；若这些不可得，她便转而使用父亲欲望的能指，以成为女人。这是因为，在与父亲的关系中，匮乏才得以可能。这种可能性为她提供了一条出路：尽管她并不拥有菲勒斯，却仍可尝试去“成为”它……为的是男性的欲望。于是，她便能时而带着某种快感，“扮演”菲勒斯，体验到当一个女人将自身化作男人欲望的对象原因（对象a）时所能引发的那种摧枯拉朽的效果。

note：“在与父亲的关系中，匮乏才得以可能”——>欲望才成为可能。

Because the mother was the first big Other of the demand, the negativization of the phallus was excluded, and thus this assumption of lack remains impossible as a metaphor. Consequently, the relationship between a girl and her mother always remains contaminated by the question of the satisfaction of maternal jouissance that the daughter either consecrates herself to, or refuses. It is why the question of “What is a woman?” cannot be posed in the mother-daughter context, where it is saturated by the maternal reference and hence leads to a dead-end.

由于母亲是欲望之要求的最初大他者，菲勒斯的否定化被排除在外，因此这种对匮乏的假设无法作为隐喻而成立。结果，女孩与母亲之间的关系始终被“母亲享乐）的满足”这一问题所污染——女儿要么献身于这种满足，要么加以拒绝。正因如此，“女人是什么？”这个问题无法在母女关系的语境中提出，因为该语境已被母性参照所饱和，从而陷入死胡同。

note：在女儿眼里，母亲被回溯建构为刚开始作为母性大他者是一个完满的大他者的，如果女儿一直陷入母女关系中，那女儿终究只是女儿，没有父亲作为一个想象父亲出现来进行“掠夺”，女儿的象征性位置无法成为“女人”。（这段同样的也能在母子关系中论证，即俄狄浦斯第一阶段的症状）

It is particularly noteworthy that after the recognition of the primacy of the phallus for both genders in 1923, Freud will always maintain that the daughter’s access to femininity can only happen when she ceases to be captive to the desire of the mother and to the auto-erotic jouissance of the relation to the mother. Only then can she become interested in the desire of the father, which necessitates a partial renunciation of infantile masturbatory jouissance. This allows a more specifically feminine relation to jouissance to emerge. In “On Femininity” (1933), Freud thus designated two arduous tasks in becoming a woman: a change of object and a change of jouissance.

尤其值得注意的是，自1923年弗洛伊德确认菲勒斯对两性均具首要地位之后，他始终主张：女儿唯有摆脱对母亲欲望的俘获，以及摆脱与母亲关系中的自体情欲享乐，才能进入女性特质。唯有如此，她才可能转向对父亲欲望的关注，而这要求她部分地放弃婴儿期手淫式的享乐。这一放弃使得一种更具女性特质的享乐关系得以浮现。因此，在《论女性特质》（1933）中，弗洛伊德将“成为女人”的过程界定为两项艰难任务：对象的转换与享乐的转换。

note：对象的转换：指女孩必须将她的力比多从最初的唯一对象——母亲那里撤回，并重新投注到父亲（以及后来的其他男性）身上；同时，随着快感区从阴蒂转向阴道，她的欲望对象也从“自体情欲的幻想对象”变成了“必须拥有菲勒斯的异性”。放弃婴儿期手淫式的享乐->放弃倒错式享乐，于是跨过了强迫症的享乐那一层。

女性的俄狄浦斯（The feminine Oedipus）

In consenting to the Oedipal metaphor, a girl fails to find a signifier that could say what a woman is. Even worse, the apparent universality of phallic signification only returns her to the radical absence of a trait of feminine identification. Left stranded by the faulty phallic signifier, she risks being swallowed up by the jouissance of the Other. There where the father fails to produce a universal law, he exposes the daughter to a return of the phallic mother’s jouissance, thus demonstrating that the paternal metaphor can only separate the daughter from the mother’s jouissance imperfectly. Since a woman is “not-all,” as determined by the father’s phallic law, she is “not-all” protected from the temptation of “mad reunions” she might experience with maternal jouissance. This can happen at the end of a love affair, or when confronted with professional failures, as well as when traversing the stages of a woman’s important biological life cycles. In these moments, she confronts a point in the structure where phallic signification lacks, leaving her without recourse in a world without bearings in which limits and forms dissolve.

在认同俄狄浦斯隐喻的过程中，女孩找不到一个能言说“女人是什么”的能指。更糟的是，菲勒斯能指看似普遍有效，却恰恰将她重新抛回女性认同特质的根本缺失之中。被这个有缺陷的菲勒斯能指所遗弃，她便面临被大他者享乐吞噬的风险。当父亲未能确立一种普遍律法时，他便使女儿暴露于菲勒斯母亲之享乐的回返之中，从而表明：父性隐喻只能不完全地将女儿与母亲的享乐分离开来。由于女人被父亲的菲勒斯律法所规定为“非全”，她也就“非全”地受到保护，无法完全抵御那种可能与母性享乐发生的“疯狂重聚”的诱惑。这种情况可能发生在一段恋情终结之际、遭遇职业挫败之时，或是在经历女性重要生理生命周期的各个阶段。在这些时刻，她直面结构中的一个点——在那里，菲勒斯能指失效了，使她陷入一个失却坐标的世界：界限与形式皆已消融，她无处可依。

note：这里的观点很有意思，父之名作为抵抗实在界最坚实的护盾，女孩是会发现其裂隙而无法被完全保护的——“非全”地被父姓律法影响，因此女孩会有极大可能重新陷入与母亲地相关的死亡融合的“death wish”中。

The girl’s primary love for the mother as phallic first big Other turns into hate when she discovers that the mother does not have the phallus. A remainder of this first attachment can create an indissoluble and ravaging bond, often taking the color of hate. A second love for the father is also disappointing because the paternal metaphor cannot say it all, leaving the girl with a persistent demand for love.

女孩对母亲的原初之爱——此时母亲作为菲勒斯化的首要大他者——在她发现母亲并不拥有菲勒斯时，便转化为恨。这一最初依恋所遗留的残余，可能形成一种无法割断且极具破坏性的纽带，往往以恨的色彩呈现。随后对父亲的第二次爱也同样令人失望，因为父姓隐喻无法道尽一切，使女孩始终怀有一种持续不断的爱之要求。

note：这里的原初之爱是需要澄清的，原文说到这个爱指向的是“母亲作为菲勒斯化的首要大他者”，因此这里的爱欲对象是“母亲大他者”，也就是在俄狄浦斯第一阶段，这种爱是一种“依恋之爱”，来源于对照料者的的天然的爱，也是诱发后俄狄浦斯后续阶段的关键要素之一。于是后文说的“恨”也可以解释了，因为“我”无法满足母亲的欲望，返回不到母亲完满状态了，于是产生了对母亲的“恨”。

The feminine Oedipus complex appears as a kind of secondary structure that only partly recovers the originary bond between the daughter and the mother and never succeeds in completely consigning it to oblivion. However, Freud never confuses this remainder of the attachment to the mother or the auto-erotic modes (sometimes very archaic) of jouissance that they entail with what could be the essence of a feminine jouissance. On the contrary, he clearly shows that the remainder of this attachment to the mother acts as a handicap for the femininity of the daughter. He remarks that the fact that the little girl turns away from the mother is a very significant step in her development. Furthermore, the passage to the paternal object is only accomplished by the passive tendencies that have remained after the “catastrophe” of the Oedipus complex (the abandonment of incest and the institution of conscience and morality).

女性俄狄浦斯呈现出一种次级结构，它仅部分地恢复了女儿与母亲之间的原初纽带，却从未能彻底将其付诸遗忘。然而，弗洛伊德从未将这种对母亲依恋的残余——以及由此所牵涉的自体情欲式享乐模式（有时极为古老）——与所谓“女性享乐”的本质混为一谈。恰恰相反，他明确指出，这种对母亲依恋的残余，反而构成了女儿女性特质发展的一种障碍。他特别强调，小女孩转向远离母亲，是其发展过程中一个极具意义的步骤。此外，向父性对象的过渡，唯有通过俄狄浦斯情结“灾难”（即放弃乱伦、确立意识与道德）之后所残留的被动倾向，才得以完成。

The remainders of the mother-daughter pre-Oedipal bond only constitute an obstacle if they remain omnipotent. The originary love for the mother may remain as a devastated and devastating place, an infernal love, a relation nourished principally by guilt and by incessant reproaches towards this Other, “the prehistoric, unforgettable other person whom no one coming after can equal” (Freud 1954: 239). The feminine clinic, and notably the clinic of hysteric women testifies largely to this terrible alienation that constitutes the love of the phallic mother in which they camp, sometimes without defection, making themselves prey to mortifying effects and to the law of the disaster that this love bears with it.

母女前俄狄浦斯联结的残余，只有在保持其全能性时才构成障碍。对母亲的原初之爱可能滞留为一片荒芜而毁灭性的场域——一种地狱般的爱，其关系主要由罪疚感和对这一大他者无休止的责难所滋养：“那个史前的、不可磨灭的他人，后来者无人能与之比肩”（弗洛伊德，1954：239）。女性临床，尤其是癔症女性的临床，大量印证了这种可怕的异化：她们固守于对菲勒斯母亲的爱之中，有时毫不动摇，从而沦为这种爱所携带的羞辱性效应与灾难法则的牺牲品。

note：因此这里的结果很好解释了，因为女人发现既然无法从母亲那里获得“女人是什么”的问题，又发现了父姓隐喻的失败，只能寄希望于回溯构建的“原初母亲”角色，这里似乎陷入了一种倒错式的执着了，但也不是倒错的（对父姓律法的）僭越。这里的女人还是不够的，因为她们依然需要一个性化的位置。

他者的绞缠：一场心理灾难（The ravage of the other: a psychic catastrophe）

Freud never used the term ravage to qualify the mother-daughter relation. However, in the course of his elaboration of feminine sexuality, he gave great importance to the high point of this primary relation of rage. This rage is ravaging for the figure of the mother and it is what allows the daughter to leave this devastated place to turn towards the father. For Freud, if there is ravage, it is the ravage of the mother. It concerns the first object of the girl’s love and its consequence is to make her exit the maternal space and push her towards the space of the father. This is indeed what we still find today in the treatment of hysteria.

弗洛伊德从未用“ravage”（绞缠）一词来界定母女关系。然而，在他关于女性特质的阐述过程中，他极为重视这一原初关系中愤怒的顶点。这种愤怒绞缠了母亲形象，也正是它使女儿得以离开这片被摧毁之地，转向父亲。对弗洛伊德而言，若存在绞缠，那便是对母亲的绞缠——它关乎女孩最初爱的对象，其后果是令她退出母性空间，并推向父性空间。这一点，我们今天在癔症的治疗中依然可见。

Freud noticed that a remainder of this bond indissolubly subsists throughout the girl’s access to femininity. Indeed, if the mother’s love initially eroticizes the body of the daughter, the mother’s hold over the sexual drives of the daughter always represents a catastrophe for the daughter’s femininity. In “On Feminine Sexuality” (1931), Freud sheds light on hysteria in this regard:

this phase of attachment to the mother is especially intimately related to the etiology of hysteria, which is not surprising when we reflect that both the phase and the neurosis are characteristically feminine, and further, that in this dependence on the mother we have the germ of later paranoia in women. For this germ appears to be the surprising, yet regular, fear of being devoured by the mother. It is plausible to assume that this fear corresponds to a hostility which develops in the child towards her mother….

弗洛伊德注意到，在女孩进入女性特质的过程中，这种联结的残余始终不可消除地持续存在。的确，如果母亲的爱最初使女儿的身体被情欲化，那么母亲对女儿性冲动的掌控，对女儿的女性特质而言始终是一场灾难。在《论女性性欲》（1931）中，弗洛伊德就此阐明了癔症的成因：

“与母亲的这一依附阶段，尤其紧密地关联着癔症的病因学。这并不令人意外，因为这一阶段与该神经症都具有典型的女性特征；此外，在对母亲的这种依赖中，还潜藏着日后女性偏执狂的胚芽。这个胚芽表现为一种令人惊讶却规律出现的恐惧——害怕被母亲吞噬。我们可以合理地假设，这种恐惧对应着孩子对母亲所发展出的敌意……”

Psychoanalysts often forget this originary bond between daughter and mother when they concern themselves with the love of the father and the fantasy of seduction that underscores it.

精神分析家们在关注对父亲的爱及其背后所支撑的诱惑幻想时，常常遗忘女儿与母亲之间这一原初的纽带。

大他者的阉割与身体的他异性（The castration of the other and the alterity of the body）

What for Freud is the specificity of the feminine experience with the mother that produces hysteric daughters? Freud discovered that while castration weighs menacingly on the sexual life of boys, the girl experiences it as an already accomplished fact. Let us leave aside the debate that ends up throwing out the baby with the bath water. Instead, let us designate more precisely the subjective consequences of this experience of castration as the girl is confronted with it. The irruption of sexual difference leaves the girl with a lack of identity when she is confronted with the lack in the Other, depriving her of an ideally phallic Other worthy of love. Freud speaks here of an impossible completion to show that privation is already inscribed at the very heart of the relation to the first Other of the demand in which subjects alienate their desire. This Other is said by Freud to be unforgettable precisely because the signifiers of the Other’s desire enter our body and in so doing eroticize it; that is to say, they cut it up into zones of drive. Lacan described the drive as the “treasure trove of signifiers” ($ ◊ D) before defining it in the 1970s as “an echo of a saying in the body” (Lacan 1975). The first seductress of the child, the mother, eroticizes the body with the signifiers of the Other. Language parasites the body with a transitivist jouissance insofar as it is not attributable to a subject or to the Other. The notion of jouissance of the Other (la jouissance de l’Autre) as designated by Lacan takes on its very meaning in the equivocation in the French preposition “de.”12 There is an equivocation between the jouissance that the subject could take from the body of the Other and the jouissance that the Other could take from the body of the subject. The identification with the imaginary phallus of the mother is correlative to this equivalence. The illusion that the girl and the mother could have something in common, bound between them indissolubly by way of their bodies, is found in this equivocation. This is why it is so ravaging for the subjectivity of the girl as well as for the mother.

对弗洛伊德而言，究竟是母亲与女儿之间怎样的女性经验，才造就了癔症的女儿？弗洛伊德发现，阉割对男孩的性生活构成一种威胁性的重压，而女孩却将其体验为一件已然完成的事实。我们暂且搁置那些最终连孩子带洗澡水一起倒掉的争论，转而更精确地界定：当女孩直面这一阉割经验时，其主观后果究竟为何？性差异的突然闯入，使女孩在面对大他者之匮乏时陷入认同的缺失——她被剥夺了一个理想化的、值得去爱的菲勒斯化的大他者。弗洛伊德在此谈及一种“不可能的完满”，以此表明：剥夺早已铭刻在主体与最初那个“要求”之大他者的关系核心之中；正是在这个关系里，主体异化了自己的欲望。弗洛伊德称这个大他者“难以忘怀”，恰恰是因为大他者欲望的能指进入我们的身体，并由此将身体情欲化——也就是说，它们把身体切割成冲动的区域。拉康曾将冲动描述为“能指的宝库”（$ ◊ D），而后在1970年代又将其界定为“身体中一句言说的回响”（Lacan 1975）。作为孩子的第一个诱惑者，母亲正是以大他者的能指来情欲化孩子的身体。语言寄生在身体之上，带来一种“传导性享乐”，这种享乐既不能归于主体，也不能归于大他者。拉康所提出的“大他者的享乐”这一概念，其意义正蕴含于法语介词“de”的歧义之中。这里存在双重含义：一是主体可能从大他者身体中获取的享乐，二是大他者可能从主体身体中攫取的享乐。女孩对母亲想象界菲勒斯的认同，正与这种等价关系相互关联。正是在这种歧义中，产生了一种幻觉：女孩与母亲之间似乎拥有某种共通之物，她们的身体被不可分割地捆绑在一起。也正是因此，这种经验对女孩和母亲各自的主体性都具有毁灭性的冲击。

note：这种绑定的后果很明显，就是精神病化。

The kernel of the ravage seems thus to be constituted by a fixation on a remainder of this modality of jouissance as jouissance of the Other. Ravage continues to occur as long as a woman has not yet disinvested the maternal Other from its ideal position, and is continually alienated in her demand.

因此，这种毁损的核心似乎就在于对这种享乐模式之残余的固着——即对（完满）大他者之享乐的固着。只要女性尚未从理想位置上撤回对母性大他者的投注，并持续在其欲望中被异化，绞缠就会不断发生。

note：这里需要和倒错严格区分开，这种更像是主动回到精神病结构。

We thus see why, in the rupture of the semblants, when the illusion that constitutes our sense of reality breaks down, when the names of the father become untied, and when what had been paternal reference points are demolished, nothing bars the ravage any longer. At such times, the jouisssance of the Other returns in the subject as unbarred, submerging the body and threatening to swallow it. This can often give the hysteric a psychotic allure and disturb the analyst, especially when witnessing the irruption of a minor hallucinatory episode (for instance, being poisoned or possessed by the mother). Importantly, this type of event risks being considered a “psychiatric case.” The psychoanalyst, however, should remain vigilant, not giving up on a diagnosis of hysteria. The term “borderline” is weak because it does not allow for distinctions to be made between hysteria and psychosis. Nevertheless, such cases are indeed a paranoid version of feminine hysteria.

因此，我们便能理解：为何一旦假相破裂——即构成我们现实感的幻象崩解、父之名松脱、那些曾作为父性参照点的东西被摧毁之时——就再无任何东西能够阻挡享乐的肆虐。此时，大他者享乐会以未被禁止的方式重返主体，淹没身体，并威胁将其吞噬。这种情况常常使癔症患者呈现出一种精神病式的表象，令分析家感到不安，尤其当目睹一次轻微的幻觉发作时（例如，感到被母亲下毒或附体）。重要的是，这类事件很容易被误判为“精神科病例”。然而，精神分析家必须保持警觉，不可轻易放弃癔症的诊断。“边缘型”这一术语是软弱无力的，因为它无法在癔症与精神病之间作出区分。尽管如此，这类个案确实可被视为女性癔症的一种偏执形式。

Just as spectacularly, in those moments where the names of the father unravel, one can witness the irruption of psychosomatic phenomena that sometimes leads the hysteric, having exhausted all the usual medical resources, to ask for an analysis.

同样引人注目的是，在那些“父之名”开始瓦解的时刻，人们可以目睹身心症状的突然爆发——这些症状有时会迫使癔症在耗尽所有常规医疗手段后，转而请求接受分析。

被阉割母亲的憎恨（The hatred of the castrated mother）

Freud did not cease underlining that the remainder of the relation mother-daughter is not love but hate. This is why he thought that the first husband would only inherit this incurable discord, often putting first marriages in danger. Freud emphasizes that this hate comes from the phase preceding Oedipus and is reinforced and exploited in the following phase. Such a hate can be quite striking and can persist throughout one’s life. What is the motivation for the little girl’s love turning into hate?

弗洛伊德不断强调，母女关系中残留下来的并非爱，而是恨。正因如此，他认为女儿的第一个丈夫只会继承这种无法治愈的不和，常常使初婚陷入危险。弗洛伊德指出，这种恨源自俄狄浦斯情结之前的阶段，并在随后的阶段中被强化和利用。此种恨意可能极为强烈，并持续终生。那么，小女孩的爱何以会转变为恨？

In order to respond to this question, Freud patiently addresses the catalogue of motives of complaints—frustrations of all sorts—that a girl could reasonably address to her mother, to finally conclude that these complaints are secondary in constructing the après-coup of the Oedipus complex. For Freud, there is no motive for this hate other than maternal castration. With maternal castration, it is the phallus as imaginary phallus that is lost. That the Other is castrated reveals that the phallus is only a lure, a postiche, confronting the girl with the flaw in the Symbolic order and depriving her of any identification in a world where nothing seems unified or real anymore, since the one that would guarantee the stability of the word and the equality of beings has disappeared. This is why there is “betrayal”—to use the term that is spoken most often by our patients—when they discover that the identificatory support that their mother offered them was only a lure. This betrayal of the Other we designate by the German vocable Versagung, which Lacan proposed to translate as “perdition” (Lacan 2001a: 353).

为了回应这个问题，弗洛伊德耐心地梳理了女孩可能向母亲提出的种种怨诉——各种各样的挫折——最终得出结论：这些怨诉在俄狄浦斯的“事后性”建构中只是次要的。对弗洛伊德而言，这种恨意别无他因，唯独源于母性阉割。通过母性阉割，丧失的是作为想象界菲勒斯的菲勒斯。大他者的阉割揭示出：菲勒斯不过是一种诱饵、一种假发，它使女孩直面象征秩序中的裂隙，并剥夺了她在这样一个世界中的任何认同可能——在这个世界里，一切似乎都不再统一，也不再真实，因为那个本应担保言词之稳固与众生之平等的存在已然消失。正因如此，当我们的来访者发现母亲曾给予她们的认同支撑不过是一种诱饵时，她们最常使用的词就是“背叛”。我们用德语词 Versagung 来指称这种大他者的背叛；拉康建议将其译为“沉沦”（Lacan 2001a: 353）。

note：大他者（父亲）对母亲的阉割——剥夺了想象菲勒斯，让婴儿转向对象征菲勒斯的认同。但是对女人而言，这个象征菲勒斯是“非全”定义的，它自身是存在问题的，于是母亲的这种匮乏/非完满导向的对父亲的认同对女儿而言反而变成了一种欺骗：“你告诉我他才是对的，但实际上他也是错的”，于是这种欺骗导向了憎恨。“Versagung”一词的“拒绝”含义也双关为对母亲引导（父之名）的拒绝。

绞缠与女性特质（Ravage and femininity）

Following Freud, Lacan took note of the radical disharmony that is the core of the mother-daughter relationship and in the 1970s he designated it with the term ravage. Lacan used this expression to introduce the formulae of sexuation in “L’étourdit” (1972), formulae written so that the phallic function is common to both sexes, thus defining masculine or feminine positioning. Let us first explore this term “ravage,” first used by Lacan as a psychoanalytic concept in 1972. This is a term from daily language that stems from the French verb ravir from the Latin rapere, which means “to take away by force.” In a figurative sense, it means “transported by admiration or joy.” The Littré dictionary defines it as “damage by violence and rapidity,” but also “destruction caused by something that is propelled, like an impetuous flow.” In the eleventh century “impetuous flow” and “ravage” were synonymous. One can thus rank different orders of ravage from the various meanings of the verb ravir. These range all the way from ravishment to swallowing up, furrowing, rapture and to devastation. It is impossible not to evoke the writing of Marguerite Duras in this context, recalling her texts The Barrage against the Pacific and The Ravishing of Lol V. Stein, to which Lacan paid written homage in 1965. Duras admitted that these works stemmed from her own ravage and she wrote them in order to make something else of it. She famously asserted during a television interview that writing is the only thing more powerful than the mother.

遵循弗洛伊德，拉康注意到了母女关系核心处那种根本性的不和谐，并在1970年代用“ravage”（绞缠）一词来指称它。拉康在《L’étourdit》（1972）中引入这一表述，以提出性化公式——这些公式被书写出来，是为了表明菲勒斯功能为两性所共有，从而界定出男性或女性的位置。让我们先来探讨一下“ravage”这个术语。拉康首次将其作为精神分析概念使用是在1972年。“ravage”本是日常语言中的一个词，源自法语动词“ravir”，而该动词又来自拉丁语“rapere”，意为“以暴力夺走”。在比喻意义上，它也指“因钦佩或喜悦而心神激荡”。《利特雷词典》（Littré）将其定义为“由暴力与迅疾造成的损害”，同时也指“由某种猛烈推进之物所引发的破坏，比如一股汹涌的激流”。早在十一世纪，“汹涌激流”与“ravage”就是同义词。因此，我们可以根据动词“ravir”的多重含义，排列出不同层级的“ravage”：从迷醉（ravissement）、吞噬、犁开、狂喜（rapture）一直到彻底的毁灭（devastation）。在此语境下，我们不可能不联想到玛格丽特·杜拉斯的写作。她的作品《抵挡太平洋的堤坝》与《劳儿·V·斯坦因的迷醉》正是拉康于1965年曾以文字致意的对象。杜拉斯承认，这些作品源于她自身的“ravage”，而她写作，正是为了从中造出别的东西。她曾在一次电视访谈中著名地断言：“写作是唯一比母亲更强大的东西。”

note：比母亲（大他者）更强大的只有圣状——但是这种强大的比较本身不也是一种菲勒斯体系下的变种吗？只不过圣状可以维系一种非对象a的确立三界平衡。

The term “ravage” not only designates a symptomatic failure of the mother-daughter relation, but is a logical effect of the “not-all,” due to the fact that a woman is “not-all” inscribed in the phallic function. The ravage is tied to a doubling between sense and absence that makes woman be “not-all” subjected to castration. As a consequence, her inscription into the phallic order will be contingent and not universal. In this sense, ravage is a logical effect of the “not-all,” stemming from a direct rapport to the default in the symbolic order.

“绞缠”一词不仅指母女关系中症状性的失败，更是“非全”的逻辑效应——因为女人“非全”被铭写于菲勒斯功能之中。“绞缠”关联着意义与缺席之间的双重性，正是这种双重性使得女人“非全”屈从于阉割。因此，她对菲勒斯秩序的铭写是偶然的，而非普遍的。就此而言，“绞缠”乃是“非全”的逻辑效应，源于主体与象征秩序之缺损直接关系。

To pose the problem in this way has immediate clinical implications: the daughter-mother bond should not be restored. The cure does not aim at repairing the harm caused by a frustrated, castrated, possessive mother, even a destructive one, by making the analyst into a “good mother.” We should add here that any analysis that has the aim of restoring this original bond relies on a denial of the structure by trapping the woman in the position of a girl.

以这种方式提出问题，具有直接的临床意涵：母女纽带不应被修复。治疗的目的，并非通过将分析家塑造成一个“好母亲”，来弥补那个受挫的、被阉割的、占有欲强的——甚至具有破坏性的——母亲所造成的伤害。在此还需补充一点：任何旨在恢复这一原初纽带的分析，都依赖于对结构的否认，它将女性困在了女孩的位置上。

note：两个方面：

  1. 好母亲：作为一种大他者凝视的结果，也是被菲勒斯功能指定的，同时母亲本身无论是完满母亲/匮乏母亲都是俄狄浦斯的结果。
  2. 恢复纽带：回溯性建构的幻想，否定了女性是出离结构定义的。

母姓绞缠（The mother-ravage）

Lacan criticized Freud for not having noticed that the feminine escapes phallic law and obligates women to pass directly through castration. Since women are not-all, they have the choice to position themselves in the phallic function or not.

拉康批评弗洛伊德未能注意到：女性特质逃逸于菲勒斯法则之外，并迫使女性直接穿越阉割。由于女性并非全部，她们可以选择是否将自身置于菲勒斯功能之中。

While often a shelter, the love of the father is, however, a risky one for the girl. When the imaginary father falls from his place and nothing can hold the world together, an abyss of unsubjectifiable jouissance opens, which necessitates the law of a castrating Other. It is here that Lacan returned to Freud to tell us that a certain number of women seem to expect more support from their mothers than from their fathers. Lacan uses the term “subsistence” here in a double sense. “Subsistence” means to stop, to stay, to endure. The etymology of the word invokes nutrition as well as the ensemble of goods necessary for existence. This relates to care-giving by way of food, implying frustration, and dependency on the mother. This maintains the daughter in the register of demand, which organizes the drive, but not the register of desire.

父之爱虽常为庇护之所，对女孩而言却暗藏风险。当想象界的父亲从其位置跌落，世界便再无维系之力，一道无法被主体化的享乐深渊随之敞开——这正亟需一个施行阉割的大他者之律法。正是在此处，拉康重返弗洛伊德，告诉我们：相当一部分女性似乎更期待从母亲而非父亲那里获得支撑。拉康在此使用了“subsistence”（持存）一词，取其双重含义：“持存”既指停驻、存留、忍耐，其词源又同时指向营养以及维系生存所必需的整套物质条件。这关联到以食物为媒介的照料，其中隐含着挫折与对母亲的依赖。此种关系将女儿维系在“要求”的界域之中——该界域组织着冲动，却尚未进入欲望的界域。

note：这里的期待母亲很复杂，也许一方面有全年母性大他者的幻觉，有照料者的依赖这样的实在界因素，也许也有临床观察出来的退行实在界要素，比如上文所说的物质条件。这里也在暗示，女性“非全”的逻辑允许她与菲勒斯秩序保持距离，从而保留了通往前象征界母性纽带的通道，也就是说，母女关系相对于母子关系会更紧密来填补父姓法则下的空缺。

However, “to subsist” is also a philosophical term that has an ontological seat, a kind of unshakable foundation that could not fail: it is not just a question of living but the question of feminine being that is posed by way of the use of this word. Lacan denounced the very feminine belief that where the signifier failed women—“She is called woman (on la dit femme) and we defamed (dit-fâmed) her” (Lacan 1998b: 85)—femininity’s real substance could be found in the relation to the mother. Here, Lacan evoked the ideas of a feminine essence, of a substantial and solid foundation for femininity that a rapport with the mother’s body could give the illusion of, not to mention the vertigo of a body-to-body. In actuality, all this covers up the abyss in which the daughter’s relation to the sex is lost.

然而，持存一词同时也具有哲学意涵，它指向一种本体论上的位置——某种不可动摇、不会失效的根基：这里所涉及的不只是“活着”，而是通过这个词所提出的女性存在的问题。拉康曾批判一种典型的女性信念：当能指对女人失效时——“人们称她为女人，而我们便诋毁了她”（Lacan 1998b: 85）——女性特质的真实实质便被认为存在于与母亲的关系之中。在此，拉康揭示了一种关于女性本质的观念：即认为与母亲身体的关联能给人一种幻觉，仿佛女性特质拥有某种坚实、实质性的基础，更不用说那种身体与身体直接相触所带来的眩晕感了。实际上，这一切不过是为了掩盖那个深渊——在其中，女儿与性的关系已然迷失。

note：第一道批判，即批判认为言说女人就是在诋毁女人，但实际上，当言说女人的时候，女人这个词语指涉的荒诞性以及试图指涉的对象相对于象征界的剩余就已经在那了；第二道批判，即认为女性实体是本体论性质的，可是当女性试图跳过象征界的建构，直接从母亲那里“继承”一种性态时，其实是会陷入无法承受的深渊（享乐）中，换言之，这种退行真的会诱发精神病的症状。

We should recall that Lacan, who had several militant feminists in analysis, addressed them on several occasions in his seminar Encore (1972), saying that there is woman only from the nature of things, which is the nature of words. Furthermore, he maintained that “one should not go into a head-spin with the anti-phallic nature of the woman which has no trace in the unconscious” (Lacan 1980).

我们应当记得，拉康曾为多位激进女性主义者做过分析，并在1972年的《再来一次》研讨班中多次对她们谈到：“女人只存在于事物的本性之中，而事物的本性即是言词的本性。”此外，他还强调：“不要因为女人具有反菲勒斯的性质就晕头转向——这种反菲勒斯性在无意识中毫无踪迹可寻。”（Lacan 1980）

It would be even more misguided and specious to look for woman in the pre-Oedipal or even less in a body-to-body experience before language. Lacan denounced, in not a very gallant manner, a number of post-Freudians who theorized the existence of a pre-Oedipal femininity: “Horney, or Deutsch for whom the tendency—when speaking of women—is to prefer ‘the voice of the body’ to the voice of the unconscious as if it was not precisely from the unconscious that the body finds a voice.” He added, “it is curious to see that, right there in analytic discourse, the excess that there is between the authority with which these woman speak gathered with the superficiality of the solutions that this speech produces” (Lacan 2001b: 463).

把女性特质到前俄狄浦斯阶段，甚至更进一步，到语言之前的那种身体对身体的体验中去寻找，就更加误入歧途、也更站不住脚了。拉康曾毫不客气地批评过一批后弗洛伊德主义者，他们主张存在一种前俄狄浦斯的女性特质。他写道：“霍妮，还有德意志——当谈到女人时，她们总倾向于偏爱‘身体的声音’，而不是无意识的声音，仿佛身体的声音恰恰不是从无意识那里获得的。”他还补充道：“奇怪的是，在分析话语内部，这些女性发言时所拥有的权威性，与其言论所产生的解决方案之肤浅之间，竟有如此明显的落差。”（Lacan 2001b: 463）

As we have previously seen, the mother is always the first seductress of the child, be it a girl or a boy, to the extent that care—the touching that she engages in with her child—is a “stimulant” (Reiz) for them. Yet, clinical practice amply reveals that what mothers—in good faith and even in the name of love—are capable of making their children endure (in particular their daughters) is absolutely without limit. The child, in effect, occupies a particular function in the economy of feminine desire due to the imaginary equivalency between child and phallus. The baby is the principal object of jouissance for feminine desire. If it weren’t for the interdiction imposed by castration, nothing would prevent a mother from using her child for her own satisfaction. This version of the “maternal instinct” opens the way for every sort of excess, for all the abuse that it is necessary to qualify as “sexual,” since the child, having been a very real object, can be played with like a play thing, a plug. The child, in trying to respond to this aspiration, is only by way of the erotization, without limits of a body.

正如我们先前所见，母亲始终是孩子的第一位诱惑者——无论孩子是女孩还是男孩——因为照料本身（即她与孩子之间的身体接触）对他们而言就是一种“刺激”。然而，临床实践充分揭示出：母亲们——出于善意，甚至以爱之名——能够让孩子（尤其是女儿）承受的东西，其限度几乎是不存在的。事实上，由于在想象界中孩子与菲勒斯具有等价性，孩子在女性欲望的经济中占据着一个特殊的位置。婴儿正是女性欲望享乐的主要对象。若非阉割所施加的禁令，没有任何东西能阻止母亲将孩子用于满足自身的欲望。这种对“母性本能”的理解，为各种过度行为打开了通道，也为一切必须被界定为“性”的滥用铺平了道路——因为孩子一旦成为真实存在的对象，就可能被当作玩具、塞子般随意摆弄。而孩子为了回应这种期待，只能通过对其身体进行无限制的性化来实现。

Women in analysis relate, sometimes with shame and sometimes with rage, memories of the practices they were subjected to as children and even adolescents under the guise of “bodily hygiene.” The rage emerges when the woman on the couch realizes that she has been as a girl an object of her mother’s jouissance. The shame arises from their realization of their own participation in the jouissance of this incestuous relation. For these women an indelible and privileged fixation of jouissance remains that, if repressed, can burden their subsequent sexual life.

接受分析的女性有时会带着羞耻、有时则带着愤怒，讲述自己在童年甚至青少年时期以“身体卫生”为名所遭受的种种行为。当躺卧在躺椅上的女性意识到，自己作为小女孩曾是母亲享乐的对象时，愤怒便油然而生；而羞耻则源于她们意识到自己也曾参与了这种乱伦关系中的享乐。对这些女性而言，一种不可磨灭且占据特权位置的享乐固着始终留存——若被压抑，便会沉重地压在其后的性生活中。

note：这种愤怒其实也说明了这些分析者是神经症结构，也就是接受了阉割。

One discovers that the fantasy of the father’s seduction can unfold during the cure, permitting the erotogenic zone concerned with the mother to serve feminine sexual jouissance instead of remaining a handicap, as Freud had put it. The fantasy of paternal seduction has the potential of inscribing the sexuality of the daughter as a subject of desire while limiting maternal jouissance. Femininity is the price of the inscription under the interdiction of the limitless jouissance of the Other. By making use of the father, the girl will subsequently be able to traverse the limit beyond the semblance and “do without the father.” Only the denial of castration can give such extraordinary force to the implacable and exclusive alienation that one sometimes encounters between a mother and a daughter.

人们发现，在分析过程中，父亲诱惑的幻想可以展开，从而使原本与母亲相关的性感带不再如弗洛伊德所说那样成为一种障碍，转而服务于女性的性享乐。父亲诱惑的幻想具有这样的潜能：它将女儿的性别身份铭刻为欲望的主体，同时限制母性的享乐。女性特质正是以这种方式被铭写在大他者无限享乐的禁令之下所付出的代价。女孩借由利用父亲这一功能，随后便能穿越假相所设下的界限，并最终“不再需要父亲”。唯有对阉割的否认，才能赋予母女之间有时所见的那种毫不妥协、排他性的异化以如此非同寻常的力量。

note：父亲的介入让女孩从母亲的孩子成为了女人，切断了母女之间无界限的融合（异化&分离），同时，在成为神经症之后，女性才有可能超越神经症，并以重新审视母女关系的方式发现一种超越父之名的防御方式——这里我理解似乎母女关系被升华为一种独特的圣状般的力量了——先利用父亲，然后穿越父亲。

Feminine jouissance is only encountered beyond the phallus, on the condition that the phallus works as a semblant. At the same time, let us highlight that the more the daughter looks for the answer to the question of woman in the relation with her mother, the more ravaging this is for her subjectivity and jouissance. Between mother and daughter there is denial of castration when the daughter gets stuck with the body of her mother unable to develop femininity. Dora understood: she scorned the jouissance of her mother’s “good housekeeping” and preferred to relay the question of femininity to her father. Often single-parent families insulate themselves in the maternal model. Today’s hysterics do not always have someone serving in the paternal function to whom they can pose this question. The analyst should be careful about not pushing a woman towards ravage, where she will only find the obscenity of maternal jouissance. There is no “practice of ravage” in the cure.

女性享乐唯有在菲勒斯之外才能遭遇，前提是菲勒斯必须作为假相运作。同时，我们必须强调：女儿越是试图在与母亲的关系中寻找“女人是什么”这个问题的答案，就越是绞缠她的主体性与享乐。当女儿固着于母亲的身体、无法发展出女性特质时，母女之间便是否认阉割的。多拉明白了这一点：她鄙弃了母亲那种“持家有道”的享乐，而宁愿将女性特质的问题转交给父亲。如今，单亲家庭常常将自身封闭在母性模型之中。当代的癔症者并不总能找到一个承担父性功能的人，向其提出这一问题。分析家必须谨慎，切勿将女性推向绞缠——在那里，她只会遭遇母性享乐的淫秽。在治疗中，并不存在所谓“绞缠的实践”。

When a patient comes to us to speak about femininity, it is often to distinguish it from the mother’s femininity. Sometimes the traits inherited from the mother are actually an obstacle to femininity. The analyst must grant a space for the analysand to be able to create her own way of being a woman.

当一位来访者来找我们谈论女性特质时，往往是为了将其与母亲的女性特质区分开来。有时，从母亲那里继承而来的特质，反而成了通往自身女性特质的障碍。分析家必须为分析者腾出一个空间，让她能够创造出属于自己的做女人的方式。

note：这也是第一步，必须摆脱和母亲的融合。

The popular idea that femininity is transmitted from mother to daughter under the pretext that they are from the same sex is a favored illusion in our modern Western society, in which patriarchy in the traditional sense no longer exists. Yet there is surely something difficult for today’s modern women, which is how to accept a limit. This limit is precisely the limit of the transmission of femininity. A woman can transmit to her daughter no more than to any other woman what it is to be a woman, any more than another woman can. Whenever a mother uses her daughter to give consistency to a “being woman,” she will make it even more difficult for the daughter to find her own path to her own femininity. This is why each time the mother is questioned during the cure of a woman, psychoanalytic ethics demand sustaining this disjunction between the mother and the woman, in order not to add to the ravage.

人们普遍认为，女性特质是从母亲传给女儿的，理由是她们同属一个性别——这种想法在我们当代西方社会中是一种广受欢迎的幻觉，而传统意义上的父权制在此早已不复存在。然而，对今天的现代女性而言，确实存在某种困难：那就是如何接受一种界限。这个界限恰恰就是女性特质传递本身的界限。一位母亲无法向自己的女儿传递“做女人”意味着什么，正如她无法向任何其他女性传递一样；任何其他女性也同样做不到这一点。每当一位母亲利用女儿来赋予自己“身为女人”以一致性时，她就让女儿更难找到属于自己的通往女性特质的道路。正因如此，在对一位女性进行分析治疗的过程中，每当涉及对母亲的质疑，精神分析伦理都要求我们维持母亲与女人之间的这一断裂，以免加剧已然存在的绞缠。

Clinically, for the subject who is its prey, the ravage can take resistant and enigmatic forms in each case. This is illustrated by the following examples: I evoke the waves of ravage that senselessly overtook a young woman, leaving her stupefied and stranded in a deep depression. Her concerned mother assiduously recruited the help of a doctor to prescribe “happiness pills.” For another, on the occasion of a pregnancy, the ravage took the form of the sensation of an alien invading her body during sleep, menacing her to the point of disintegration. For this analysand, the repeated irruption of a surge of hatred against her own unborn daughter was an extreme irrational violence that made her fear the worst and left her stranded, feeling like an empty bag. For yet another analysand, an edema of Quincke, a rapid, dramatic, inflammation of skin, menaced her each evening at dinnertime, pushing her inexorably towards anorexia and leaving her swollen and ravished.

临床上，对那些成为其猎物的主体而言，这种绞缠在每个个案中都呈现出顽固而晦涩的形式。以下例子可资说明：我想到一位年轻女性，她被一波波毫无意义的绞缠所吞噬，陷入深深的抑郁，茫然无措、动弹不得。她忧心忡忡的母亲不遗余力地请来医生，只为开些“快乐药丸”。另一位分析者在怀孕期间，绞缠则表现为一种异己之物趁她入睡时侵入身体的感觉，威胁着要将她彻底瓦解。对她而言，反复涌现的、针对自己未出世女儿的仇恨冲动，是一种极端非理性的暴力，令她恐惧最坏之事的发生，只觉自己如一只空瘪的袋子，被弃置一旁。还有另一位分析者，每到晚餐时分便遭逢奎因克水肿——一种迅速而剧烈的皮肤炎症——的威胁，这迫使她一步步滑向厌食症，身体肿胀，精神亦被掏空。

男性绞缠（Man-ravage）

Lacan defines the function of the sinthome as a “supplement” (suppléance) to make up for the lack of a rapport (Lacan 2005: 101). The sinthome corrects the knotting precisely where its failure occurred. He adds, “there is a rapport only where there is a sinthome. It is the sinthome that allows the other sex to be tolerated. I take the liberty to say that the sinthome is the sex to which I do not belong, that is to say, a woman. A woman is for every man a sinthome. For what a man is for a woman, we must find another name, since the sinthome is characterized by non-equivalence. A man is for a woman, anything you please, an affliction, worse than a sinthome, even a ravage” (Lacan 2005: 101).

拉康将圣状的功能界定为一种增补，用以弥补性关系之匮乏（Lacan 2005: 101）。圣状恰恰在纽结失效之处进行修正。他进一步指出：“唯有存在圣状的地方，才有所谓的关系。正是圣状使得另一性别得以被容忍。我冒昧地说：圣状就是我不所属的那个性别，也就是说，女人。对每个男人而言，女人就是一个圣状。至于男人对女人意味着什么，我们必须另寻一个名称，因为圣状的特征在于非对等性。对女人来说，男人可以是任何东西——一种折磨，比圣状更糟，甚至是一种绞缠。”

note：因为男人是对女人的排除——让女人与象征界断链。

In Lacan’s formulations in this late seminar, woman is a sinthome for a man and man is a ravage for a woman. There is a radical distinction between ravage and sinthome. If the sinthome is what allows to make up for what does not stop not being written, it seems that in the sexual relation with a man, insofar as a woman enters into it as not-all (pas-toute), her jouissance will be other than phallic jouissance, and therefore she will not find the union that she expects.

在拉康晚期研讨班的论述中，女人对男人而言是一种圣状，而男人对女人而言则是一种绞缠。这里，绞缠与圣状之间存在着根本性的区分。如果说圣状的功能在于对“无法停止不被书写之物”进行增补，那么当女人以其“非全”的姿态进入与男人的性关系时，她的享乐便不同于菲勒斯享乐，因此她无法获得自己所期待的那种结合。

The solitude of the jouissance has not left her many opportunities to take on a ravaging aspect, something we have a number of clinical testimonies of. Often after the daughter has experienced ravage with the mother, the woman will experience ravage with the man. Freud noted this ravaging dimension in a woman’s sexual relations with a man. However, when confronted with this, Freud makes of the phallic cause something dear to women. According to him, it is the principal motive of their affliction, “a hostile bitterness” (Freud 1918), towards the man who took their virginity. Freud tells us we should never ignore the impact of this bitterness in the relation between the sexes.

享乐的孤独并未给她留下太多机会去展现其绞缠的一面，而这一点我们已有诸多临床见证。通常，在女儿与母亲之间经历了这种绞缠之后，女性随后又会在与男性关系中经历同样的绞缠。弗洛伊德注意到了女性在与男性的性关系中所呈现的这种绞缠维度。然而，当面对这一现象时，弗洛伊德却将菲勒斯的原因视为女性所珍视之物。在他看来，这正是她们痛苦的主要动因——即对那个夺走自己童贞的男人怀有一种“敌意的苦涩”（Freud 1918）。弗洛伊德提醒我们，绝不可忽视这种苦涩在两性关系中的影响。

According to Freud, it is not so much the narcissistic wound (Freud 1918: 205) felt during the first sexual encounter but the raging phallic plaint present in the stormy mother-daughter relation that contaminates a woman’s first amorous relationship with a man. Defloration “unleashes an archaic reaction of hostility” (Freud 1918: 208). Thus, the first husband will simply inherit the bad mother-daughter relationship. Freud asserts that, as a rule, “second marriages so often turn out better than first” (Freud 1918: 207). Freud writes, “I think it must strike the observer in how uncommonly large a number of cases the woman remains frigid and feels unhappy in a first marriage, whereas after it has been dissolved she becomes a tender wife, able to make her second husband happy. The archaic reaction has, so to speak, exhausted itself on the first object” (Freud 1918: 203).

根据弗洛伊德的说法，污染女性与男性首次恋爱关系的，并非主要源于初次性经验中所感受到的那种自恋性创伤（Freud 1918: 205），而是母女之间激烈关系中所蕴含的、狂暴的菲勒斯式哀怨。破身“引发了一种古老的敌意反应”（Freud 1918: 208）。因此，第一位丈夫只不过继承了这段糟糕的母女关系。弗洛伊德断言，通常而言，“第二次婚姻往往比第一次更美满”（Freud 1918: 207）。他写道：“我认为，任何观察者都会注意到，在数量异常之多的案例中，女性在第一次婚姻里始终冷淡且感到不幸；而一旦这段婚姻解除，她便能成为一位温柔的妻子，令第二任丈夫幸福。可以说，那种古老的反应已经在第一个对象身上耗尽了”（Freud 1918: 203）。

This clinical remark Freud made in 1918 illuminates the entire field of the psychology of the amorous life of the woman. This field is often neglected by psychoanalysts, who prefer to relegate it to the hysteric woman’s neurotic dissatisfaction. Is it the kernel of maternal ravage that is reactivated in a woman’s amorous passion, or is there something else at stake in the ravage with the man she loves, the man who provides her with sexual jouissance? Freud notes that under the auspices of marriage, the expectation and the act do not match up in coitus. He adds that the woman finds tenderness only in an illicit liaison that must remain secret; the only one where she can act on her own influence and not by the influence of others (Freud 1918: 203). Freud recognizes a disjunction in women’s difficulty in choosing between the man who gives her his name, i.e., the husband, and the man who gives her sexual jouissance, i.e., the lover. If this painful demand exists in women, namely that only one man should give them both, isn’t it simply because these demands are within themselves antinomic?13 When a woman chooses a man for his name, identity, and social status, this phallic realization will take place at the expense of feminine jouissance. Many women show day to day satisfaction in being the wife of a man and the mother of his children; this works as long as the fantasmatic or real seducer does not appear to disturb the conjugal peace and open the space of ravage. As we see in some hysterical patients, as soon as a husband is suspected of taking a mistress he suddenly becomes an object of fantasy, a sexual seducer. Here the effects of ravage cannot be simply reduced to the reality of the infidelity or the presence of the other woman. The conjugal betrayal is inflected by an earlier maternal disappointment.

弗洛伊德1918年做出的这一临床观察，照亮了女性情爱生活的整个心理领域。这个领域常被精神分析家所忽视，他们更倾向于将其归结为癔症女性的神经症性不满。那么，在女性的情爱激情中被重新激活的，究竟是母性创伤的核心？还是说，在她与所爱之人的关系中、在那个给予她性享乐的男人身上，另有所涉？弗洛伊德指出，在婚姻的庇护下，性交中的期待与实际行为并不一致。他进一步补充道：女性唯有在一段必须保持隐秘的婚外情中才能找到柔情；唯有在这种关系里，她能凭自己的影响力行事，而非受他人影响（Freud 1918: 203）。弗洛伊德由此识别出一种分裂：女性难以在“给予她名分的男人”（即丈夫）与“给予她性享乐的男人”（即情人）之间做出抉择。如果女性确实存在这样一种痛苦的要求——希望同一个男人同时给予她名分与性享乐——难道不正是因为这两种要求本身是相互矛盾的吗？当一个女人选择一个男人，是因其姓名、身份与社会地位，这种菲勒斯式的实现便以牺牲女性享乐为代价。许多女性在日常生活中满足于做某位男人的妻子、做他孩子的母亲；只要幻想中或现实中那个诱惑者尚未出现，以搅乱婚姻的平静并打开绞缠的空间，这种状态就能维持下去。正如我们在某些癔症患者身上所见：一旦丈夫被怀疑有了情妇，他突然就变成了幻想的对象、一名性诱惑者。在此，绞缠的效果不能简单地还原为通奸的事实或“另一个女人”的存在。婚姻的背叛被早先的母性失望所折射。

Why does the relation to the beloved man, the one she experiences jouissance from, evince ravaging effects beyond the ones that she gets from being the object of his desire? Why does man become woman’s ravage? We might find the answer in considering the complex and rich question of feminine jouissance. Woman has the Other as her partner, and in Lacan’s formulation, a very particular Other, that in his development of the formulae of sexuation he calls the “l’hommoinsun” (Lacan 1973: 35), one who will not exist, much like Lacan will also say of “The woman.”14 At the same time, it is possible to say that the hommoinsun exists by pure contingency, a contingency that one finds specifically in love, that is, by being the one who supports feminine jouissance. Also spelled au-moins-un (“at-least-one” who is not castrated), as the partner of feminine jouissance, he is identified with the figure of exception. This is why, in love, whereas the man believes he creates the woman, actually, he puts her to work supplementing the failure of complementarity so that the “at-least-one,” this partner for feminine jouissance, will ex-sist. Lacan writes ex-sist with a dash to underline the au-moins-un’s enigmatic exterior, peripheral existence, since the au-moins-un exists most often by being denounced as “not it.”

为何女性与所爱的男人之间的关系——她从中体验到享乐的关系——会带来比仅仅作为他欲望对象时更为毁灭性的后果？为何男人竟成了女人的“绞缠”？答案或许就藏在女性享乐这一复杂而丰饶的问题之中。女人以大他者为伴侣；而在拉康的表述中，这是一种极为特殊的大他者。在他对性化公式的展开中，拉康称之为“l’hommoinsun”（拉康，1973：35）——一个不会存在的人，正如他也会说“女人不存在”一样。但与此同时，我们亦可说，“l’hommoinsun”恰恰凭借纯粹的偶然性而存在，这种偶然性尤其体现在爱之中：他正是那个支撑女性享乐的人。“l’hommoinsun”也写作“au-moins-un”（“至少有一个”未被阉割者），作为女性享乐的伴侣，他被认同为那个例外的形象。正因如此，在爱之中，尽管男人以为自己创造了女人，实际上他却是在驱使她去增补互补性的失败，以便让这个“至少有一个”——即女性享乐的伴侣——得以“外-在”。拉康特意用连字符写成“ex-sister”，以强调“au-moins-un”那神秘的外部性与边缘性存在：因为“au-moins-un”往往正是通过被指认为“不是它”而存在的。

note：也就是男人幻想女人作为“原父”的形象成为了例外。

The au-moins-un is the figure that subtends feminine jouissance. Valuable insofar as he is denounced, he is at the same time all the more enjoyed since there will never be a man qualified to be the exception she is looking for. She can thus nourish the precious dissatisfaction she enjoys—recognizing in her man the one from whom she wants to get an enjoyment beyond his castration. It is in this regard that the hysteric refuses to give the value of the universal to the particular. She would rather go on strike than compromise in allowing jouissance to condescend to desire.

“至少一”（不受阳具逻辑限制）是支撑女性享乐的形象。他之所以珍贵，恰恰在于他被揭穿；而正因为他永远无法成为她所追寻的那个例外——那个足以配得上例外地位的男人——她反而更能从中获得享乐。于是，她便能滋养那种她所珍爱的不满：在自己的男人身上，她认出的正是那个她指望能带来超越其阉割之局限的享乐的人。也正是在这个意义上，癔症主体拒绝将特殊赋予普遍的价值。她宁可罢工，也不愿妥协，让享乐屈尊俯就于欲望。

note：这里女人“反而更能从中获得享乐”是符合癔症欲望逻辑的，也就是限制自己的欲望满足。也同时，女人发现了男人匮乏以后才更会索取，她在此触摸到了“大他者”的匮乏，从而获得了一种神秘的快感。也因此，女人受困于欲望不满足，却也因为欲望不满足而“满足”。

If, as a consequence, the hysteric’s quest for the exception is ravaging, it is because she unceasingly repeats, verifying again and again the impossibility of finding the “one” who would finally match up with her jouissance. She thus bears the entire weight of the hétéros and this is ravaging for her. The man’s desire, on the other hand, when he makes a woman the object of his jouissance, uses a phallic yardstick for measuring that can be all the more obliterating when his desire inscribes and reduces her to a simple phallic exchange value. Here we often see that at the heart of the relation to the beloved man who gives her sexual jouissance we find the replication of the feeling of “being nothing for him” or of being rejected by her mother.

因此，癔症主体对“例外”的追寻之所以具有毁灭性，是因为她不断重复着一种验证：反复确认根本找不到那个能真正契合她享乐的“唯一之人”。于是，她独自承担了全部“他异性”的重负，而这对她而言是毁灭性的。另一方面，当男人将女人视为其享乐的对象时，他的欲望会动用一把菲勒斯的标尺去衡量她；而当他的欲望将她铭写并简化为单纯的菲勒斯交换价值时，这种衡量就愈发具有抹除性。我们常常看到，在与那位能给予她性享乐的所爱男人的关系核心处，重现的恰恰是那种“对他而言自己什么都不是”或“被母亲拒绝”的感受。

note：不像男人的条件是有一个例外，女人的条件恰恰是“没有例外”——不存在主体不受阳具逻辑限制。于是女人寻找例外的过程就是“毁灭性”的，因为女人永远不能找到例外。女人不断发现：果然这个男人什么都不了我，这个正对应儿童时期的其母亲被剥夺的幻想：一方面，女人会发现男人什么都不是，但另一方面，她还发现了自己什么都不是——正如她无法扮演母亲的菲勒斯一般，她不可能真正成为男性伴侣的关键菲勒斯。简单来说，女人通过不断寻找男人来确定自己的存在，但是总是失败——性别问题正是存在问题。

One hypothesis we could propose on the basis of the insistence of this scenario in clinical work with women would be that by way of man-ravage the hysteric protects herself from a more devastating ravage, where she would experience a subjectless pain impossible to name or identify.15 She thus allows herself to be divided by the jouissance that a man takes by way of her, making a partner of the solitude she encounters, while the real union will never materialize (Lacan 1973: 23).

我们可以基于这一场景在女性临床工作中的反复出现提出一个假设：歇斯底里者通过“男人的绞缠”来保护自己，以免陷入一种更具毁灭性的绞缠——在那里，她将体验到一种无主体的痛苦，这种痛苦无法命名，也无法辨认。因此，她容许自己被男人经由她所获得的享乐所分裂，把遭遇的孤独当作伴侣，而真正的结合却永远不会实现（拉康，1973：23）。

In so doing, she experiences the painful realization that the sexes will not meet, and that the two modes of jouissance will never coincide; Achilles will never join with the tortoise, finally making a “whole” where she could belong once and for all. In fact, woman will never join up with the One, since, insofar as she supports the Other sex, she will just be able to differentiate herself from the other sex in producing the One au moins (at-least-One/subtracted One), ad infinitum.

通过这种方式，她痛苦地意识到：两性无法真正相遇，两种享乐模式永远无法重合；阿喀琉斯永远追不上乌龟，终究无法构成一个让她一劳永逸归属其中的“整体”。事实上，女人永远无法与“太一”合一，因为只要她承担起作为大他者性别的位置，她就只能通过不断生产出“至少一”来与另一性别相区分，如此无限延续下去。

Insofar as she is “not-all” phallic, a woman can encounter the limit where all supplementation fails. This limit reveals that there is no order to existence, and no partner who could respond to restore order. There is no rapport between man and woman where what never ceases to not be written would finally be written or where subjectivization would finally hold. This is the structural reason why women are more affected by ravage than men.

由于女性“并非全部”属于菲勒斯秩序，她可能遭遇这样一个界限：在那里，一切增补都失效了。这一界限揭示出，存在本身并无秩序可言，也不存在一个能回应并恢复秩序的伴侣。男女之间并不存在一种性关系，使得那“永不停止不被书写之物”最终被书写出来，或使得主体化最终得以稳固。这正是女性比男性更容易遭受“绞缠”的结构性原因。

第十四章：伍尔夫、乔伊斯与“疯狂”的主体（Woolf, Joyce and the “mad” subject）——Juliet Flower MacCannell

Virginia Woolf and James Joyce: two “mad” authors of the early twentieth century. One a woman, one a man; one English, one Irish. Each equally revolutionary for their “stream of consciousness” literary techniques; each equally refusing the premises of psychoanalysis. Woolf declined analysis, fearing harm to her creativity; Joyce rejected it for his schizophrenic daughter Lucia: she was merely “telepathic,” he said.1 Each regarded writing as freeing them from the conditions that ordinarily shape the child, its cast of mind, its ego. Which elements configuring their egos—family, language, gender, or the political discourse that encircled them—explain why Woolf’s rebellious ego ends with despair and “madness” (and her suicide), while Joyce’s opens jubilant possibilities for art and thought?

弗吉尼亚·伍尔夫与詹姆斯·乔伊斯：二十世纪早期两位“疯狂”的作家。一位是女性，一位是男性；一位是英国人，一位是爱尔兰人。两人都因“意识流”文学技法而同样具有革命性，也都同样拒绝精神分析的前提。伍尔夫拒绝接受分析，生怕损害自己的创造力；乔伊斯则为患有精神分裂症的女儿露西亚拒绝了分析，他说她只不过是“有心灵感应”罢了。两人都认为写作能让他们从那些通常塑造孩童、塑造其心智与自我的条件中解脱出来。究竟是哪些建构其自我的元素——家庭、语言、性别，还是环绕他们的政治话语——能够解释，为何伍尔夫那反叛的自我最终走向绝望与“疯狂”乃至自杀，而乔伊斯的自我却为艺术与思想敞开了令人欢欣的可能？

The Lacanian psychoanalysis developed in Freud’s wake confirmed his thesis that the unconscious is linked to language—to what is said and what cannot be said. The ego makes every effort to repress the unconscious, barring by means of its power of formalization, from entering linguistic expression. Woolf and Joyce both broke radically with such ego-centered policing of language, to be sure, but how did their particular writing egos permit this? And what was the effect of this rupture on their own psyches?

在弗洛伊德之后发展起来的拉康派精神分析证实了他的论断：无意识与语言紧密相连——关乎说出的与无法说出的。自我竭尽全力压抑无意识，凭借其形式化能力，阻止无意识进入语言表达。诚然，伍尔夫和乔伊斯都彻底打破了这种以自我为中心的语言管控，但他们各自的写作自我是如何允许这一切发生的？而这种断裂又对他们自身的精神结构产生了怎样的影响？

第十八章：精神分裂式书写与疯狂的引擎（Schizophrenic writing and the engine of madness）——Manya Steinkoler

There is a medieval saying that “madness is more a device or engine, an ingenium, than a destiny.” In the Latin for engine, ingenium, we find the root of genius, as well as the connotation of a natural disposition as we read the root, gen, appearing in “engendering” and “congenital.” Destiny connotes a narrative quest, a drama with a definable “end,” and concerns the long-term link between psychoanalysis and tragedy, with the unconscious becoming conscious, Oedipus, awakening to his horror, and tearing out his eyes. Ingenium is a creative ability to escape from destiny, like Ulysses and Diomedes’ ingenious Trojan horse: it is an invention that both is and allows for the unexpected. The horse was a construction, a work of masterful engineering, related to another definition of ingenium, “to engineer.” An ingenious invention, we can think of a building, a birth, or in the case I will discuss today, a writing.

中世纪有句俗语：“疯狂与其说是一种命运，不如说是一种装置或引擎，一种巧思。”在拉丁语中，引擎对应的词是ingenium，我们能从中找到“天赋”的词根，它也带有天生倾向的意味，因为词根gen同样出现在“孕育”和“先天”中。命运意味着一种叙事性的追寻，一出有着明确“结局”的戏剧，它关乎精神分析与悲剧的长久纽带，关乎无意识转化为意识，关乎俄狄浦斯在惊骇中觉醒并刺瞎双眼。而巧思则是一种逃离命运的创造能力，就像尤利西斯和狄俄墨德斯那巧妙的特洛伊木马：这项发明本身出人意料，又为意外留出了空间。木马是一种建构，一件精妙的工程杰作，这呼应了ingenium的另一层含义“去设计”。说到巧妙的发明，我们可以想到一座建筑、一次诞生，或者就我今天要讨论的案例而言，一种书写。

A gifted schizophrenic—an actor, a writer, and a homeopathic doctor—would visit the hospital where I worked like a great prince taking the spa waters for a brief respite from his royal duties. It is by recourse to a specific text of his that I will discuss how showing the “engine” at work can be a kind of “genius,” one that differs from the work of sublimation in neurosis. This essay contemplates Lacan’s notion of the infinite line, not as “repressed” or unconscious, but as Real. In this regard, the delusion (délire) is itself a production, an invention, literally off (de) the tracks (lira), that exceeds the furrows of the Real. Basking in the Real completely, delusion can often seem utterly unreadable, opaque, impenetrable. Running off the tracks, or inventing new ones, this “madness,” a delusional construction, is nevertheless a work, and, at times even—and such a debate is far beyond the scope of this essay—a work of art. Madness is an art, we could hypothesize, that gives form to this “engine.” In this case, it reveals our “natural disposition” as highly unnatural, twisted, rambling, careening, circling, and thus more human. This artful madness exposes a fundamental senselessness of the human engendered by language itself.

一位天赋异禀的精神分裂症患者——他身兼演员、作家和顺势疗法医师——常来我工作的医院，就像一位显赫的亲王来泡温泉，好从繁重的王室事务中偷得半日清闲。我将借他的一篇特定文本，探讨展示“引擎”的运作如何能成为一种“天才”之举，这不同于神经症中的升华工作。本文探讨拉康的“无限线”概念，不把它看作“被压抑的”或无意识的东西，而是看作实在界。就此而言，谵妄本身就是一种生产和发明，字面意义上它是“脱轨”的，溢出了实在界的沟壑。完全沉浸在实在界中，谵妄往往显得彻底不可读、晦暗且无法穿透。这种“疯狂”作为一种谵妄建构，无论是脱离旧轨道还是发明新轨道，终究是一种创作，有时甚至——尽管相关争论远超本文范围——是一件艺术品。我们可以假设，疯狂是一门赋予这台“引擎”以形式的艺术。在此，它揭示出我们的“自然禀赋”其实极不自然，扭曲、散漫、摇摆、盘旋，因而更具人性。这种充满艺术感的疯狂，暴露出人类由语言本身所滋生的根本的无意义。

What form can be given to this engine? Lacan tells us: a circle. He writes in Le Sinthome, “But the Freudian unconscious … it is just what I said, to know the rapport, the rapport that there is between a body that is a stranger to us and something that makes a circle, an infinite line, that in any event is the one (the D.I. infinite line), one (the circle) equivalent to the other (the D.I.) and something that is the unconscious” (Lacan 1976; my translation).1

这个装置能呈现出什么形态呢？拉康告诉我们：是一个圆。他在《圣状》中写道：“至于弗洛伊德的无意识……就是我说的，去认识那种关系，即一个对我们来说全然陌生的身体，与某种勾勒出圆或无限线的东西之间的关系。无论如何，这一个（无限直线）和那一个（圆）是等价的，而某种东西正是无意识。”

“Le Jeu mémoire du dieu-serpent Mehen” (“The Game of Memory of the God-Serpent Mehen”) is a text that attempts to render the infinite line or circle as the hole of the Real; the writer knows about it. He is inside.2 This text is the synopsis of a screenplay that has not been written. The author—I will call him Ixidor, the Basque version of Isidor, which means “gift of Isis”—is a supremely functional, widely read, brilliant schizophrenic. He took a screenwriting course and was delighted to “finally understand” how screenplays are constructed. “Screenplays have a pie shape and events happen in a certain order and at a certain time,” he explained to me, pleased with the new understanding the screenwriting class had afforded him. Ixidor would insist on the importance of this discovery. “Form” is an imaginary container, an ego, lacking in psychosis and in schizophrenia in particular. The “pie shape” where events “took place in time,” was a “find” for Ixidor. We note that the pie (tarte in French) shape is round, like the Mehen “board game” from ancient Egypt he will choose for his subject matter. He is literally matching the round form. Ixidor would write nearly 300 pages, “obsessively,” he told me. Then, carefully cutting his work down to fit the pie model he had learned in class, he would “now know where to put things that happened, by following the formula.” He was thus able to reduce the nearly 300 pages down to eleven dense, almost unreadable single-spaced pages. Those eleven pages are the subject of this essay.

《蛇神梅亨的记忆游戏》这篇文本试图将无限的线或圆呈现为实在界的洞孔；作者对此心知肚明。他身在其中。这篇文本是一部尚未写出的剧本的剧情梗概。作者——我称他为伊克西多尔，这是伊西多尔的巴斯克语版本，意为“伊西斯的礼物”——是一个社会功能极好、博览群书、才华横溢的精神分裂症患者。他上了一门编剧课，并很高兴自己“终于弄懂”了剧本是怎么构建的。“剧本呈派状，事件按特定顺序在特定时间发生，”他向我解释道，对编剧课带给他的新认知感到满意。伊克西多尔反复强调这一发现的重要性。“形式”是一个想象界的容器，一个自我，这在精神病中，尤其是在精神分裂症中是缺失的。事件“在时间中发生”的这种“派状”结构，对伊克西多尔来说是个“新发现”。我们注意到，派的形状是圆的，就像他选作主题的古埃及梅亨“棋盘游戏”一样。他简直是在严丝合缝地匹配这种圆形形式。伊克西多尔写了将近300页，他告诉我，写得“强迫性十足”。随后，他仔细删减作品以契合课上学到的派状模型，他“现在知道该把发生的事情放在哪了，只要套用公式就行”。就这样，他硬是把近300页压缩成11页密密麻麻、几乎读不下去的单倍行距文稿。这11页文稿正是本文探讨的主题。

Ivoripuion

  6. 6. 第二部分：疯狂中的方法：思考精神病（THE METHOD IN MADNESS：Thinking psychosis）
    1. 6.1. 第六章：你无法选择发疯——内斯托尔·布朗斯坦（“YOU CANNOT CHOOSE TO GO CRAZY”）
    2. 6.2. 第七章：精神病的治疗与当代精神分析——让-克洛德·马勒瓦尔（TREATMENT OF THE PSYCHOSES AND CONTEMPORARY PSYCHOANALYSIS）
    3. 6.3. 第八章：精神病性转移——让·阿卢什（PSYCHOTIC TRANSFERENCE）
      1. 6.3.1. 转移：一与多（Transference: the one and the many）
      2. 6.3.2. 转移的能指（The signifier of transference）
      3. 6.3.3. 进行转移／以转移的方式定位（To transfer/to position transferentially）
      4. 6.3.4. 让知识被知晓（Letting knowledge be known）
    4. 6.4. 第九章：躁郁性精神病的特异性（THE SPECIFICITY OF MANIC-DEPRESSIVE PSYCHOSIS）——达里安·利德（Darian Leader）
    5. 6.5. 第十章：忧郁症-未弃置客体（MELANCHOLIA AND THE UNABANDONED OBJECT）——拉塞尔·格里格（Russell Grigg）
      1. 6.5.1. “‘忧郁症’，而非‘抑郁’”（“Melancholia,” not “depression”）
      2. 6.5.2. 弗洛伊德的“哀悼与忧郁”（Freud’s “Mourning and Melancholia”）
      3. 6.5.3. 哀悼（Mourning）
      4. 6.5.4. 忧郁（Melancholia）
      5. 6.5.5. 忧郁症和强迫性神经症（Melancholia and obsessional neurosis）
      6. 6.5.6. 忧郁症和除权（Melancholia and foreclosure）
    6. 6.6. 第十一章：疯狂、主体性和镜子阶段：拉康和梅洛庞蒂（MADNESS, SUBJECTIVITY, AND THE MIRROR STAGE：Jasper Feyaerts and Stijn Vanheule）——Jasper Feyaerts and Stijn Vanheule
      1. 6.6.1. 介绍（Introduction）
      2. 6.6.2. 疯狂的隐喻（The metaphysics of madness）
      3. 6.6.3. 自然主义态度与亲历体验（Naturalistic attitude and lived experience）
      4. 6.6.4. 拉康读瓦隆（Lacan reads Wallon）
      5. 6.6.5. 梅洛庞蒂读拉康和瓦隆（Merleau-Ponty reads Lacan and Wallon）
      6. 6.6.6. 主体性和疯狂：拉康VS梅洛庞蒂（Subjectivity and madness: Lacan versus Merleau-Ponty）
    7. 6.7. 第十二章：自恋神经症和非性化创伤（NARCISSISTIC NEUROSIS AND NON-SEXUAL TRAUMA）
    8. 6.8. 第十三章：她彻底疯了：癔症、女人与精神分析家（SHE’S RAVING MAD：The hysteric, the woman, and the psychoanalyst）——克洛德-诺埃尔·皮克曼（Claude-Noële Pickmann）
      1. 6.8.1. S(Ⱥ)：拉康的情书（S(Ⱥ): Lacan’s love letter）
      2. 6.8.2. 为何是无而非有（Why is there nothing rather than something?）
      3. 6.8.3. 女性的俄狄浦斯（The feminine Oedipus）
      4. 6.8.4. 他者的绞缠：一场心理灾难（The ravage of the other: a psychic catastrophe）
      5. 6.8.5. 大他者的阉割与身体的他异性（The castration of the other and the alterity of the body）
      6. 6.8.6. 被阉割母亲的憎恨（The hatred of the castrated mother）
      7. 6.8.7. 绞缠与女性特质（Ravage and femininity）
      8. 6.8.8. 母姓绞缠（The mother-ravage）
      9. 6.8.9. 男性绞缠（Man-ravage）
    9. 6.9. 第十四章：伍尔夫、乔伊斯与“疯狂”的主体（Woolf, Joyce and the “mad” subject）——Juliet Flower MacCannell
    10. 6.10. 第十八章：精神分裂式书写与疯狂的引擎（Schizophrenic writing and the engine of madness）——Manya Steinkoler
